# Provenance rounds (issue #1)

Dated, append-only log of the primary-source attribution rounds: one entry per
source and round, in the order they were run (the template of issue #1,
section 4). Read by no code; the machine-readable state is in
`sources/databases/<Src>/primary_references.csv`, `Bib/pending_citations.csv`,
`Bib/scite_checks.csv` and `reports/warnings_citations.md`.

## 2026-10-04 -- Kiorboe_2013 (pilot A0, Stage 0 of issue #1)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (`R/library/citations/`, branch `issue-1-citation-tooling`).

- Parser: `BodyMass_Kiorboe_2013.r` keeps the Table A1 reference number as `ref_keys` (633 records, 0 NA, 17 of the 18 keys; reference 11 cited by no kept record).
- `--init --verify --queue` (live Crossref + OpenAlex, 31 requests, cached under `sources/citations_cache/`): certain 10 (two-service agreement), pending 6 (weak_match 2 Falk-Petersen 1981 at title_sim 0.922 because the compilation misspells "zooplakton", 14 Putt & Stoecker 1989 at 0.917 without the leading "An", 9 Kremer 1976 thesis, 13 Omori & Ikeda 1984 book; grey_literature 5 Ikeda 1974 Memoirs; ambiguous 18 Williams & Robins 1982 against an "Oceanographic Literature Review" abstract reprint), not_found 1 (7 Ikeda & Hirakawa 1998; Consensus identified the paper under its full title, no DOI exists). The compilation's own Crossref record has no deposited reference list (no closed-world candidates).
- Screening: Scite MCP returned its monthly quota error (250 calls, resets 2026-11-01) on every call; Consensus confirmed 13 of the 14 accepted and candidate DOIs by title (`consensus-mcp`, `unchecked`); the Kremer thesis DOI 10.23860/diss-2825 is in neither service (`none`). Crossref `update-to` / OpenAlex `is_retracted`: no notice on any DOI. **Scite re-screening of the 14 DOIs is due after 2026-11-01.**
- Owner decisions (2026-10-04, `Bib/pending_citations.csv`): 2 -> `1` (10.1016/0022-0981(81)90065-4); 14 -> `1` (10.4319/lo.1989.34.6.1097); 18 -> `1` (10.1007/bf00397044); 9 -> `nodoi` (@phdthesis Kremer 1976, University of Rhode Island; the digitised thesis's DOI, dated 2025 at Crossref, not adopted); 7 -> `nodoi` in the published form (parsed_* set to Ikeda, Hirakawa & Imamura 1998, Plankton Biology and Ecology 45: 31-44; recorded in `notes`); 5 and 13 deferred (decision empty, pending). The ten Consensus-only certain DOIs proceed (owner decision: Crossref/OpenAlex flags were consulted; re-screen in November).
- `--apply-queue --bib`: 14 entries in `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_PrimaryCitations.bib` (RefManageR parses all), Menden-Deuer & Lessard 2000 reuses the curated `Menden-Deuer:2000aa` / `MendenDeuer_2000`. `--sheet` (dry run, then real): tab `BM_primary_citations` created with headers, 15 rows appended, snapshots `Bib/BM_primary_citations_snapshot.csv` (15 rows) and `Bib/BM_citations_snapshot.csv` (430 rows).
- Full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off): `TaxonBodyMass.csv` and `TaxonBodyMass_GenusLevel.csv` unchanged; `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 120,978 rows for 38,566 species; `reports/warnings_citations.md`: 0 problems, 2 pending references, 30 distinct primary CiteIDs; Kiorboe_2013 coverage 106 of 224 record links (47.3%) of 100 accepted species; 461 of 633 records resolved.
- Design decisions taken by the owner on this round: theses and books keep queuing (no auto-accept); a `:year=YYYY` suffix on a candidate or `doi:` decision overrides the service record's year (recorded as `year_override`, noted in the entry); near-miss strong matches (0.90-0.93 with all flags) and abstract-reprint runner-ups keep queuing; `default_provenance_type` stays `unknown` until a compilation is ingested; equation references (Brocher_etal_2025 -> Sohlstrom et al. 2018; Hishi_etal_2019 -> Tanaka 1970, Petersen 1975) are to be verified like primary references with role `equation` in a later round (TODO in the registry notes); keys of sources without a `primary_references.csv` read `uningested`; unused reference-list entries are reported, not verified; every uncertain case is collected and put to the owner in one batch at the end of a round, never settled by a default.
- Still open for Kiorboe_2013: references 5 (Ikeda 1974, Memoirs of the Faculty of Fisheries, Hokkaido University 22: 1-97, 153 records -- in no service; the Hokkaido University repository HUSCAP holds the Memoirs under handles, no DOI known) and 13 (Omori & Ikeda 1984 book, 19 records -- exists without a DOI); options `nodoi` from the stated fields, `doi:` if one is found, `drop`.

## 2026-10-04 -- Hebert_etal_2016 (pilot A0, Stage 1 of issue #1)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-1-pilot-hebert`, rebased on main c260d28 with Hudson_2013).

- Parser: `BodyMass_Hebert_etal_2016.r` keeps the `Ref.dm` codes as `ref_keys` (723 records, 721 keyed, 53 distinct keys, 39 multi-code records); the regression codes 18 and 20 are excluded with their rows and a guard stops the parser if they reach a kept record. Reference code 1 (CIEE freshwater zooplankton database, unpublished; owner's role `database` decision) turned out to be cited only on rows that also carry 18/20, so it is cited by no kept record and no role edit was needed; 145 of the 198 list entries are unused.
- `--init --verify --queue` (live Crossref + OpenAlex, cached under `sources/citations_cache/`; the compilation's Crossref record deposits no references, so no closed-world candidates): certain 21 (two-service agreement), pending 23 (weak_match 15, ambiguous 4, grey_literature 4), not_found 9 (below_threshold); 32 rows queued. The weak matches come from the list's missing volume/pages columns (only the journal string can satisfy the container/volume/pages condition) and from Crossref titles with footnote digits, HTML tags or inserted authorities.
- Screening: Scite MCP quota exhausted until 2026-11-01 (not called); Consensus confirmed all 21 certain DOIs and the candidates of 70, 72, 73, 75, 84, 179 by title/author/year/journal (24 rows added to `Bib/scite_checks.csv`, `consensus-mcp`, `unchecked`). Crossref `update-to` / OpenAlex `is_retracted`: no notice. The one-page erratum of Ikeda et al. 2001 (10.1007/s00227-001-0743-z) is not linked by Crossref `update-to`; recorded in `notes` of reference 75. **Scite re-screening due after 2026-11-01.**
- Owner decisions (2026-10-04): `1` for 70, 73, 72, 84, 75, 31, 172, 178, 7, 24, 26, 27, 32, 41, 43, 45, 49, 65, 148; `2` for 179 (Mayzaud 1976 part IV); `nodoi` for 74 (Ikeda, Hirakawa & Imamura 1998, the work already approved as Kiorboe_2013 reference 7); deferred (decision empty): 69, 3, 181, 4, 25, 29, 153, 157, 173 (print-only references without a DOI), 9 (Smirnov: Crossref 1962 vs the list's 1964) and 71 (Ikeda 2013 unpublished data; see the comparison below).
- Tooling change (owner decision 2026-10-04): a `nodoi` decision reuses the DOI-less entry another source's decision already produced when the first surname, year and normalised title agree (`MatchingNoDOIEntry()` in `build_bib.r`, used by `--bib`; tests in `R/library/tests/test_citations_bib.R`): Hebert 74 -> `Ikeda:1998aa` / `Ikeda_1998` instead of a duplicate `Ikeda:1998ab`.
- Reference 71 check: the 40 Hebert records citing "Ikeda 2013, Marine zooplankton metabolic measurements (unpublished data)" are replicate individual dry masses of 5 copepods; their per-taxon means equal the species values of Ikeda_2014's ESM tables S1/S2 (attributed there to Ikeda et al. 2001 and 2007) for Acartia tonsa (mean 0.0072 mg), Euchaeta marina (0.291), Metridia pacifica (0.094) and Undinula vulgaris (0.185); Calanus propinquus differs (Hebert mean 0.807 mg vs Ikeda 1.04). Decision left to the owner.
- `--apply-queue --bib`: 52 entries in `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_PrimaryCitations.bib` (RefManageR parses all; 1 curated key reused: Menden-Deuer:2000aa from Kiorboe); Hebert_etal_2016 rows with bibcite 42 (4 shared with Kiorboe_2013: Ikeda:1989aa, Ikeda:1998aa, Omori:1969aa, Tande:1982aa). `--sheet` dry run then real: 38 rows appended to `BM_primary_citations` (tab 15 -> 53 rows), snapshots updated (`BM_citations` 430 rows).
- Full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off): `TaxonBodyMass.csv` and `TaxonBodyMass_GenusLevel.csv` byte-identical; `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 121,160 rows for 38,566 species; `reports/warnings_citations.md`: 58 distinct primary CiteIDs, 13 pending or not-found references, 1 pre-existing problem (Hudson_2013 has no Bibcite / CiteID row yet, from PR #62); Hebert_etal_2016 coverage 483 of 629 record links (76.8%) of 133 accepted species; 582 of 723 records (80.5%) and 124 of 159 taxa resolved at the frame level.
- Still open for Hebert_etal_2016: 69 (Ikeda 1974 Memoirs, 95 records, shared with Kiorboe_2013 reference 5), 71 (40 records), 3 (17 records), 181 (8), 4 (4), 25 (4), 9, 29, 153, 157, 173 (1 record each); options `nodoi` from the stated fields, `doi:` if one is found, `drop`.

## 2026-10-04 -- Herberstein_etal_2022 (pilot A0, Stage 1 of issue #1; hop 2)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-1-pilot-herberstein`, merged with main f1eee70 after the Hebert pilot).

- Parser: `BodyMass_Herberstein_etal_2022.r` keeps the hash key `h:<sha1-8>` of the normalised `fullReference` as `ref_keys` (`ParseInRowCitations()`; 2,856 records, 0 NA, 193 keys; one in-text form, "Terblanche, Klok, & Chown, 2004", stands for two papers, which is why the full text and not the in-text form is hashed). The in-row parser gained `labels`: the record's `inTextReference` becomes the reference's `note` (`in-text: ...`) so that the hash keys stay readable (`reflist_specs` `intext_col`).
- Hop 2 (issue #1, 1.3) implemented in `BuildProvenance()`: a resolved reference whose CiteID, bib key or DOI is a registered source label is an intermediate compilation (`MatchIntermediateLabel()`); the intermediate's own resolved references for the species give `compiled_via_compilation` rows at hop 2 with `via_cite_id` (`IntermediateReferences()`), a `self` reference of the intermediate gives `compiled_from` citing it, and otherwise the intermediate is the terminal citation (`compilation_terminal`, owner decision). First case: Chown et al. 2007 (244 records, 8 ant species, Chown's own Appendix S1 measurements) -> 7 accepted species `compilation_terminal` at `Chown_etal_2007` until Chown is ingested. `Chown:2007aa` received `doi` and `volume` in the curated bib and the source `Citation.bib` (owner-accepted hand edit) so that the DOI route and the key reuse of `--bib` recognise it.
- Tooling fixes found on this round: `ExtractDOI()` truncated DOIs at the first `)` (52 of the 155 in-row DOIs, `10.1016/0300-9629(77)90123-4` -> `...(77`) and later at `<` (SICI DOIs); parentheses and angle brackets are now kept and only an unbalanced closing bracket is dropped. OpenAlex now limits keyless use to 1,000 requests a day and answers the rest with 429 and a day-long Retry-After, for which httr2 slept ("Waiting 83003s for retry backoff"): `CachedGET()` treats such a 429 as an exhausted quota (classed condition `citations_quota`, host flagged for the session), `VerifyPrimaryReferences()` leaves the affected references unverified and reports them, `OPENALEX_API_KEY` is sent when set (never stored: the cache key and the stored URL drop it). The first verify of this round (two pilots on one day) left 60 references unverified; they were finished with the owner's key.
- `--init --verify --queue` (compilation's Crossref record deposits 237 references, 223 with DOI): certain 176 (doi_resolves 151, two_service_agreement 25), pending 11 (ambiguous 2, weak_match 5, doi_mismatch 3, retracted 1 = a correction notice), not_found 6 (below_threshold); 17 rows queued.
- Screening: Scite MCP quota exhausted until 2026-11-01 (not called); Consensus confirmed by title 179 accepted and candidate DOIs (`Bib/scite_checks.csv`, `consensus-mcp`, `unchecked`); not among its three results: Bauchot & Stephan 1966, Pirlot & Stephan 1970, Hails 1983, Baudinette et al. 2000, Nevo & Shkolnik 1974, Krog & Monson 1954, El-Nouty et al. 1978 (no row; their in-row DOIs resolve at Crossref with matching author and year); Warnke 1908, Mlikovsky 1990 and 2003, Jensen & Nielsen 1975 not found. Crossref `update-to` / OpenAlex `is_retracted`: no retraction; Pasquet et al. 2018 has a correction (10.1007/s10164-018-0574-8, 2019), kept in `editorial_notice`. **Scite re-screening due after 2026-11-01.**
- Owner decisions (2026-10-04): `1` for h:00d71a7c Seymour 1998 (CUP DOI 10.1017/s0952836998001125, the Wiley DOI of the same article not adopted), h:0af12421 Boyce 2020 (journal, not the bioRxiv preprint), h:0b6a8709 Bailey 1993, h:17705f14 Putnam 1982, h:d43fcc97 Krol 1994, h:96a8db76 Pasquet 2018 (correction, not retraction); `nodoi` for h:346450e6 Warnke 1908, h:92c79f30 Crile & Quiring 1940 (volume 40, pages 219-259 supplied by the owner), h:89c47794 and h:9b3042d8 Mlikovsky 1990 and 2003, h:bd7628e5 Jensen & Nielsen 1975, and -- owner rule: DataCite-only or Crossref-unknown DOIs (Zenodo/BHL deposits, Oxford's Auk DOIs) are never adopted, the entry is built from the stated fields -- h:680b909e MacMillen et al. 1977, h:ba9a2986 Hadley et al. 1981, h:ce39c3a4 Chaplin 1982, h:e337855a Anderson 1996 (the deposit DOIs are kept in `notes`). The full author lists and journal titles of the `nodoi` entries were taken from the raw citations into `parsed_*`. Also applied: Hebert_etal_2016 reference 71 (Ikeda 2013 unpublished data, 40 records) -> `drop` (owner policy: unpublished data cite the compilation only).
- `--apply-queue --bib`: 242 entries in `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_PrimaryCitations.bib` (RefManageR parses all; 2 curated keys reused: Menden-Deuer:2000aa, Chown:2007aa); Herberstein_etal_2022 rows with bibcite 191. `--sheet` dry run then real: 191 rows appended to `BM_primary_citations` (tab 53 -> 244 rows), snapshots updated (`BM_citations` 430 rows).
- Full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off): `TaxonBodyMass.csv` and `TaxonBodyMass_GenusLevel.csv` byte-identical; `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 121,328 rows for 38,566 species; `reports/warnings_citations.md`: 239 distinct primary CiteIDs, 14 pending or not-found references (2 Herberstein, 12 Hebert/Kiorboe deferrals), 1 pre-existing problem (Hudson_2013 has no Bibcite / CiteID row yet, PR #62); Herberstein_etal_2022 coverage 2,376 of 2,390 record links (99.4%) of 1,525 accepted species, 181 distinct primary CiteIDs; 2,840 of 2,856 records and 1,819 of 1,830 taxa resolved at the frame level; the 7 Chown species are `compilation_terminal` (224 record links).
- Still open for Herberstein_etal_2022: h:3cfad141 Nielsen 1986 (15 records; the source's DOI is cut short, `10.1016/0022-1910(86)90131-`; Crossref holds 10.1016/0022-1910(86)90131-9 with the same title, author, journal, volume and pages, Consensus confirms the paper) and h:fc0ce1f8 Campbell & Hochachka 2000 (1 record; the in-row SICI DOI `10.1644/1545-1542(2000)081<0578:TBAMOT>2.0.CO;2` was truncated at `<` by the old `ExtractDOI()`); options `doi:<DOI>` (re-verified at apply), `nodoi`, defer.

## 2026-10-04 -- Chown_etal_2007 (tier A2, issue #63)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-63-chown-references`; `ParseNatureStyle()` added to `normalise_citation.r` for the list's Nature reference style, 4 tests).

- Source: the owner replaced the Word supplement `fec1245_supmat.doc` by the publisher's PDF `fec1245_supmat.pdf`; `parse_chown_supmat.py` (PyMuPDF) regenerates Appendix S1 (244 ant records, identical to the first parse), Appendix S2 (391 rows; the first parse of the .doc had missed the 44 rows with a blank Method or Wing-status cell, added on the owner's decision: 36 identified records, no species added and no mass changed because all are species already present through Ehnes_etal_2011 / Makarieva_2008 and collapse as copies; 27 species and 25 genus rows gain the label) and `references.csv` (115 numbered entries, verbatim apart from line joins; keys `*` = "Chown lab, unpublished data." and `S1` = the Appendix S1 heading and footnote). Checks: numbers 1-115 without gap, all cited, first citations in numerical order.
- Parser: `BodyMass_Chown_etal_2007.r` keeps the S2 superscript number or `*` and gives the S1 ants the key `S1`; 581 of 581 records keyed, 115 keys. Owner decisions (2026-10-04): the 44 missed rows are added; S1 and `*` are role `self` (measured by Chown's group).
- `--init --verify --queue` (live Crossref + OpenAlex; OpenAlex's free per-IP budget was exhausted by the parallel pilot runs, the owner's API key, read from `OPENALEX_API_KEY`, unblocked it): certain 87 (two-service agreement), pending 22 (weak_match 14, ambiguous 7, single_service 1), not_found 4, self 2. The compilation's own Crossref record deposits 71 references (66 with DOI). Decision table put to the owner in one batch.
- Screening: Scite MCP quota exhausted; Consensus confirmed all 55 accepted DOIs not yet screened by the earlier rounds (title, first author, year, journal; `consensus-mcp`, `unchecked`); Crossref `update-to` / OpenAlex `is_retracted`: no notice on any of the 87. **Scite re-screening of the Chown DOIs is due after 2026-11-01.**
- Owner decisions (2026-10-04, `Bib/pending_citations.csv`): 13 typo near-misses -> `1` (9, 17, 18, 27, 36, 52, 59, 65, 79, 80, 100, 103, 115); duplicate-DOI pairs -> the Wiley 10.1111 form (19, 38, 41 -> `1`; 96, 49 -> `2`); 8 Slater 1927 part II -> `1`; 95 Gade & Auerswald 1998 journal form -> `1`; 64 Klok & Chown "in press" -> `1` (2005 paper); `nodoi` for 16, 63, 84 (Zenodo-only DOI; DataCite policy), 101 (same work as Herberstein_etal_2022 h:bd7628e5, key `Jensen:1975aa` reused by `MatchingNoDOIEntry()`) and 30 (chapter title supplied by the owner); author lists of the DOI-less entries in BibTeX form from the raw citations. Policies recorded: DataCite-only DOIs -> `nodoi`; unpublished data -> `drop`. Also applied for Herberstein_etal_2022: h:3cfad141 -> `doi:10.1016/0022-1910(86)90131-9` (= Chown 97, `Nielsen:1986aa` shared) and h:fc0ce1f8 -> `doi:10.1644/1545-1542(2000)081<0578:TBAMOT>2.0.CO;2` (`Campbell:2000aa`).
- `--apply-queue --bib`: 26 + 2 decisions applied; 320 entries in `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_PrimaryCitations.bib` (RefManageR parses all), Chown 113 rows with bibcite. `--sheet` (dry run, then real): 77 new rows for Chown_etal_2007 (36 already present), 1 for Herberstein_etal_2022; `BM_primary_citations` now 322 rows (snapshot committed).
- Full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off): species and genus tables identical to main; provenance 121,364 rows, 313 distinct primary CiteIDs, 12 references pending / not found overall (none of Chown's), 0 unverified; Chown_etal_2007: 537 record links of 295 species, 286 `compiled_from` (hop 1) + 251 `measured_in_source`; Herberstein_etal_2022's Chown rows: 7 species `compiled_from` citing Chown, 3 `compiled_via_compilation` (hop 2). `reports/warnings_citations.md`: the one remaining problem (Hudson_2013 without a Bibcite) predates this round.
- Tooling notes from this round: re-running `--queue` after recording decisions but before `--apply-queue` appends duplicate open rows for the decided keys (run `--apply-queue` first); `verify_services.r` honoured a 23-hour `Retry-After` from OpenAlex before the API-key support arrived.


## 2026-10-04 -- Brose_etal_2018 and Brose_2005 (web-level attribution, issue #64; tier B / A2 of issue #1)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-64-brose-web-provenance`); owner decision 2026-10-04: the records of a food web cite the web's own citation (GATEWAy `link.citation`, Brose 2005 `Link reference`), the masses being population means of the web (#29).

- Parsers: `BodyMass_Brose_etal_2018.r` keeps `ref_keys` = key(s) of the record's `link.citation` through `WebReferenceKeys()` (`R/library/foodweb_units.r`) and `references.csv` (26 keys for the 23 citation strings of the 290 webs: first author + year, a string naming two works gives two keys; verbatim entries of the NEE SI "References of Table 1", DOIs from the #14 audit, `owner_review` flags for aliases, theses and chapters); 268,901 kept records, all keyed, 24 keys cited. `R/library/data_retrieve.r` keeps the Brose 2005 study key(s) from `brose2005_references.csv` (21 keys for 20 studies, verbatim Ecological Archives E086-135 literature cited); the cached live frame predates the keys, so they take effect at the next `DataRetrieve = TRUE` run (issue #69); the round verified the 12 cited keys against a regenerated frame (475 taxon rows).
- `--init --verify --queue`: OpenAlex refused every keyless request on 2026-10-04 (free daily budget shared by the network's IP; the Herberstein pilot's `OPENALEX_API_KEY` support and quota fail-fast now on main); with the owner's key: Brose_etal_2018 certain 13 (`doi_resolves`), pending 8, not_found 3; Brose_2005 certain 1 (Pinnegar 2003, two-service agreement), pending 8, not_found 3. New tooling from this round: `owner_review` / `review_col` (a reference the list marks is never auto-accepted), the `service_unavailable` reason, per-label `primary_references_<Label>.csv` in a shared folder, 60 s request timeout with retry.
- Screening: Scite quota exhausted until 2026-11-01; Consensus confirmed 23 candidate DOIs (`consensus-mcp`, `unchecked`); the Jacob et al. 2015 chapter DOI in neither service (`none`). Crossref `update-to` / OpenAlex `is_retracted`: no notice. **Scite re-screening due after 2026-11-01.**
- Owner decisions (2026-10-04, `Bib/pending_citations.csv`): unpublished contributions are not cited as primary references, the compilation is the citable source (`drop`: Kroll; Ledger, Edwards & Woodward; Ruess; Rayner; Jacob, Brey & Mintenbeck; Dell -- contributor names kept in `notes` and the READMEs); Mendonca_2018 `doi:10.1371/journal.pone.0200066` (the web paper; SI Table 1: Vinagre et al., unpublished); Layer_2010 `1` (Adv. Ecol. Res. 20-streams study, not the Glob. Change Biol. paper the SI cites); Thompson_2017 `1:year=2017`; Jacob_2011, Jacob_2015, Jonsson_2004, Woodward_2005 `1`; CattinBlandenier_2004 `1:year=2004` in both labels (digitised thesis DOI dated 2025); Nsiku_1999, Opitz_1996, Sutherland_1989, Ulrich_2001, Ulrich_1999 `nodoi`; Simberloff_1969 `drop` (species lists, no body sizes); Woodward_2001 `doi:10.1046/j.1365-2656.2002.00669.x` (the 2002 body-size paper the file's size column credits). Policy for later: DataCite-only DOIs -> `nodoi`.
- `--apply-queue --bib --sheet`: 22 decisions applied (11 per label; Ulrich_1999 queued and decided `nodoi` after the first apply, the queue showing exactly that one new row); 27 entries added to `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_PrimaryCitations.bib` (347 entries in all; `Jonsson:2005aa` and `Cattin-Blandenier:2004aa` shared by both labels; no curated key reused), 27 rows appended to `BM_primary_citations` (22 + 5; tab 322 -> 349 rows), snapshots updated. Tooling fixes on the way: a Crossref record whose first author element has no name (the Neuchatel thesis) takes the first named author for key minting; `NormaliseSurname()` drops initials folded into a family name ('A. Piechnik') and title-cases all-capital surnames ('MULDER') for keys and CiteIDs. Known weak entries from Crossref metadata: Piechnik et al. 2008 (volume 0, early-view page string, split author names) and Mulder & Elser 2009 (capitalised names); the owner may replace them with curated entries.
- Full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off): `TaxonBodyMass.csv` and `TaxonBodyMass_GenusLevel.csv` unchanged; `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 121,692 rows for 38,566 species; `reports/warnings_citations.md`: 0 problems, 12 pending references overall (none of Brose), 334 distinct primary CiteIDs; Brose_etal_2018: 252,216 of 268,901 records (93.8%) cite an accepted reference, 1,820 of the 2,081 record links of 1,753 species (87.5%) carry a primary CiteID (`compiled_from`, hop 1; the report's own measure: 90.4% of 168,179 record links), 261 links of the two rejected works stay `rejected`; Brose_2005 0% until the frame is regenerated (#69; 355 of 475 taxon rows on the regenerated frame).
- Side findings: the SI also cites Sechi et al. 2015 for the Dutch soil webs (omitted from `link.citation`; noted, not keyed); the GATEWAy README's per-web action count corrected (165 keep / 18 convert_dry / 107 drop); DataRetriever cache drift filed as #69.

## 2026-10-04 -- Wilman_etal_2014 (EltonTraits mammals and birds, issue #65; tier A1 of issue #1)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-65-wilman-mammals`); owner decision 2026-10-04 to ingest the mammal half of EltonTraits, whose every row carries `Ref_` codes resolvable in `MamFuncDatSources.txt`.

- Parser: `BodyMass_Wilman_etal_2014.r` reads both halves under one label (13,157 -> 12,673 rows after the PrimScale drop below): mammals 4,041 species-level rows kept of 5,400 (812 Genus/Family Average rows, Ref_2/Ref_3, SpecLevel 0, and 547 Ref_178 phylogenetically imputed rows dropped through `DropImputed()`), `ref_keys` = the comma-separated `Ref_` codes (59 keys cited; one row cites two); birds keep the `BodyMass-Source` word as key (Dunning08 8,620, HBW volumes 9, Other 3). `references.csv`: the 176 `MamFuncDatSources.txt` entries verbatim after cp1252 decoding (the issue's 190 was a line count; nine entries span several lines) plus the nine bird keys. Owner decisions on the data (2026-10-04): the 484 PrimScale bird rows (EltonTraits' own length-scaled estimates) are dropped as imputed (148 bird species whose only value they were and 35 genera leave the output; 268 bird values change); the two Ref_165 (Fooden 1963) Lagothrix rows are kilograms and are multiplied by 1,000 (Lagothrix lagotricha had fallen to 62 g and out through the range filter); `Wilman_etal_2014 -> Smith_2003` registered at 0.001 log10 (74% of 3,361 shared species identical, 95% within 0.001, 98% within 0.0105; the strict tolerance leaves the 153 species whose MOM version differs from v10.2 independent and avoids the 0.0105 sibling pairs that collapsed AnAge and McCoy bird values into Wilman) and `-> Jones_2009` at 1e-6 (481 of 481 identical); Mustela itatsi (Ref_121, 57 g vs 300-565 g), the three Sicista of Ref_146 (6.4-7.2 g; PHYLACINE agrees, COMBINE's 61.8 g is the odd value) and the 29 birds left with a single VertNet value by the PrimScale drop (Uratelornis chimaera 5 g, impossible, CRITICAL; the rest SUSPICIOUS) are kept and registered in `audit/flagged_species.csv` (`source_conflict_2026-10-04`). Four of the "five birds gained from main" were renames of the `taxon` column (the species exist on main under their accepted names; the VertNet synonym became the input name when the PrimScale row left); Uratelornis chimaera is the one real gain (range-filtered on main against the 226 g PrimScale estimate).
- `--init --verify --queue` (live Crossref + OpenAlex with the owner's `OPENALEX_API_KEY`, 1 request/s; the keyless run of the same day stopped on OpenAlex's spent daily budget, Retry-After 81,840 s): 67 keys, certain 11 (two-service agreement), pending 8, not_found 48 (46 below_threshold, 2 no_candidates); the compilation's own Crossref record has no deposited reference list. Nine below_threshold rows were exact-title near-misses the regex parser missed (citations without a year before the title, title_sim 0 against the right candidate).
- Screening: Scite quota exhausted until 2026-11-01; Consensus confirmed 17 accepted and candidate DOIs (`consensus-mcp`, `unchecked`); Davis 1970 (Tomopeas ravus, 10.2307/1378474), Reid 2009 (OUP book DOI) and Wolff 1985 (10.2307/3565572) in neither service (`none`). Crossref `update-to` / OpenAlex `is_retracted`: no notice. **Scite re-screening due after 2026-11-01.**
- Owner decisions (2026-10-04, `Bib/pending_citations.csv`): `1` for Ruedi 1995, Vilela 2006, Rickart 2002, Mullin 2004, Bonvicino 2003, Dowler 2000, Wolff 1985, Breed 2000 (OUP DOI) and Reid 2009; `2` for Lunde 2003; `1:year=2008` for the Dunning handbook (Crossref says 2007); `nodoi` for Hershkovitz 1990 and 1998, Emmons 1999 (no Crossref-registered Novitates 3279 DOI exists), Simmons 1998 and Musser 1992 (Bull. AMNH; the raw text names one author each and no volume for Musser -- Heaney / 211 and Voss still to be confirmed by the owner), Garbutt 1999, Menkhorst & Knight 2004, Bonaccorso 1998, the six HBW volumes (del Hoyo, Elliott, Sargatal & Christie, Lynx Edicions; years from the volumes' imprints) and the six Animal Diversity Web accounts (URLs in `notes`; the tool has no url field); `drop` for the five 'Reference lost' keys (20 records) and 'Other' (unpublished or lost references cite the compilation only); DEFERRED: Ref_146 (a 1995 Nauka handbook reduced to question marks in the archive file, 12 records; the owner may identify it) and 18 species descriptions in journals Crossref does not cover (Ref_138, 141, 144, 145, 147, 148, 152/176, 153, 154, 157, 159, 161, 167, 169, 170, 172, 177). Roles: Ref_117 Smith 2003, Ref_58 PanTHERIA, Dunning08 and the HBW volumes `compilation` (hop 2 through the registry once Smith_2003 and Jones_2009 are ingested; until then `compilation_terminal` at the intermediate). The `parsed_*` of the 20 nodoi rows were filled from the raw citations by the agent and approved with the decisions. DataCite-only DOIs take `nodoi` (owner policy).
- `--apply-queue --bib --sheet`: 37 decisions applied; the curated `Smith:2003aa` and `Jones:2009aa` entries gained `doi` fields (the Chown precedent) so that the Wilman rows reuse them (`Smith_2003`, `Jones_2009` CiteIDs, the hop-2 match) instead of minting `Smith:2003ab`; 387 entries in `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_PrimaryCitations.bib` (RefManageR parses all; Dunning minted as `Dunning-Jr:2007aa` / `DunningJr_2007` from Crossref's surname and year, entry year 2008 by the override); 42 rows appended to `BM_primary_citations` (tab 349 -> 391 rows), snapshots updated.
- Full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off): 38,566 -> 38,422 species against main fe128d1 (gained Leimacomys buettneri, Orthogeomys matagalpae, O. thaeleri, Uratelornis chimaera; lost 148 PrimScale-only birds), 414 values changed (268 birds, 146 mammals; max 0.43 log10, median 0.017), 3,774 mammal species carry Wilman_etal_2014 (3,239 collapse into Smith_2003, 315 into Jones_2009, 201 independent), 8,444 birds; genera 10,725 -> 10,692 (gained Leimacomys, Uratelornis; 35 lost; 348 means changed). `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 125,016 rows for 38,422 species; `reports/warnings_citations.md`: 1 problem (Hudson_2013's Sheet row, from main), 31 pending/not-found references overall, 376 distinct primary CiteIDs; Wilman_etal_2014 coverage 99.5% of 12,287 record links (42 of 67 references resolved: 12,087 links `compilation_terminal` -- 8,433 at Dunning 2008, 3,184 at Smith_2003, 461 at Jones_2009, 9 at HBW volumes -- and 141 `compiled_from` the 31 articles, books and web accounts; 35 links not_found/deferred, 23 rejected).
- Still open for Wilman_etal_2014: Ref_146 and the 18 deferred species descriptions; the Heaney / Bull. AMNH 211 and Voss confirmations for Ref_173 and Ref_160; hop 2 of the 3,645 Smith_2003 / Jones_2009 links awaits the ingestion of those two sources (#4 for MOM).
- Follow-up (2026-10-04, after PR #84, branch `issue-65-followups`): owner confirmations entered -- Ref_173 `Musser and Heaney`, Bulletin of the AMNH 211; Ref_160 `Simmons and Voss`; the Dunning handbook's key and CiteID hand-set to `Dunning:2008aa` / `Dunning_2008` in `primary_references.csv` and `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_CitationCiteIDs.csv` (the stale `Dunning-Jr:2007aa` entry left the regenerated bib). `--bib` only, no `--sheet`: the three `BM_primary_citations` rows written on 2026-10-04 (`DunningJr_2007 | \citep{Dunning-Jr:2007aa}`, `Musser_1992`, `Simmons_1998`) are stale and are for the owner to edit by hand in the Sheet (the tooling is append-only); the snapshot follows at the next `--sheet`.

## 2026-10-04 -- Lemoine_2026 (new source, issue #76; primary, no reference list)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-76-lemoine-2026` from main edcffcf). Phases A and B of the ingestion; no PR, no `--init`.

- Source: Lemoine, Lechopier, Marichal, Molin & Coulis (2026) Eur. J. Soil Biol. 130:103854 (CC BY 4.0; Crossref issued 2026-09; `update-to` none, OpenAlex `is_retracted` FALSE), calibration data set of an image-based biomass method: 1,163 soil macroinvertebrates of Martinique collected alive and weighed fresh within 24 h on a microbalance, then freeze-dried (921) and scanned. The deposited file (CIRAD Dataverse doi:10.18167/DVN1/SCH4AV v1.0, md5 `d5670a45...`) replaces the owner's comma-separated copy, a re-export in which 36 fresh-mass cells were 10x or 100x the deposited value and one was unparseable (README). Only the weighed fresh masses are used; the dry masses, the ImageJ measurements and the paper's regression estimates are not (no image-derived or modelled value enters). Parser `BodyMass_Lemoine_2026.r`: 12 dry-only rows filtered; `DropImputed()` 22 rows (2 cf. labels), 271 rows (36 morphospecies codes and genus-/family-level placeholders, dropped in the parser because the raw-name vocabulary cannot read the genus-less codes), 58 rows (larvae and nymphs, `larve = TRUE`); 800 records of 56 labels kept, 55 accepted species (52 new to the database), 41 new genera. Two misspellings added to `fix_misspellings.r` (*Chodromorpha*, *Scolopocryptos*); the pipeline rules handle the rest (subgenus fold, GBIF synonyms, one fuzzy match). No source copies these values: no edge in `Bib/source_dependencies.csv`.
- Provenance: registered as `primary` / `measured_in_source` (tier F; registry 81 labels, primary 17; `test_citations_provenance.R` counts updated). The owner expected shipped references, but the data set cites no study for any mass: every value is the authors' own weighing, `Citation.bib` holds the paper itself, and the paper's 49 references concern the method (length-mass allometries, preservation effects). No `references.csv`, no `reflist_specs` entry, no `primary_references.csv`, no `--init --verify --queue` (nothing to verify; a one-row `self` list would reproduce the registry default); no OpenAlex or Crossref call through the tooling, so no quota was used. Provenance table: 55 rows `measured_in_source` at hop 0, 800 record links, `Lemoine_2026` listed in `reports/warnings_citations.md` as a label without a `BM_citations` row and Bibcite until the owner adds the entry (as for `Hudson_2013`).
- Screening: Scite not called (quota exhausted until 2026-11-01). Consensus (`mcp__consensus__search`, one call on the title) returned the paper as its first result (Lemoine et al. 2026, European Journal of Soil Biology, 0 citations); no row added to `Bib/scite_checks.csv`, which screens accepted primary-reference DOIs and this source has none.
- Open for the owner (hand-back of 2026-10-04): the `BM_citations` row and BibDesk key for `Lemoine_2026`; whether the 149 kept rows with `larve` NA (not recorded) and the deliberately sampled juveniles among the `larve = FALSE` rows are acceptable as field individuals (alternatives: drop the NA rows, or keep only the largest individuals); whether the two cf. identifications and the dry-only individuals should be used; the eight GBIF synonym placements; the misspelling-table entries.

## 2026-10-04 -- Leahy_2025 (new source, issue #75; primary data, self reference)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-75-leahy-2025`, from main edcffcf, merged with main 5637575 after the Chown_etal_2007 round).

- Source: Leahy et al. (2025) PNAS 122(29): e2501541122, Dryad 10.5061/dryad.d7wm37qb7, `mr_ant_525.csv`: 2,805 individual worker ants (214 colonies, 114 names, six locations in south-eastern Australia) weighed by the authors after their metabolic assays (wet mass, mg). The deposit was expected to ship references for its sources; it does not: the body masses are the authors' own measurements and the deposit's only references (Economo et al. 2018, Zomer et al. 2022) concern the phylogeny and the aridity classes. Registered as class `primary`, default `measured_in_source`.
- Parser: `BodyMass_Leahy_2025.r` keeps the 57 binomials (1,557 records; the 57 'nr.', 'sp.' and coded morphospecies names leave with 1,245 rows; the three duplicate rows of individ.code 4123 are dropped through `DropImputed()`), wet mg -> g, n = 1, `ref_keys` 'self' on every record.
- `--init --verify --queue` with `references.csv` holding the one entry (the paper itself, written 'This study'): 1 self reference (role `self`, status `self`), 0 service calls, 0 queue rows. No Crossref/OpenAlex request, so no quota use. Consensus confirmed the publication itself by title, authors, year and journal (one search; no `Bib/scite_checks.csv` row, since no primary DOI was accepted).
- Full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off): `TaxonBodyMass.csv` 38,566 -> 38,616 species (+50 new ant species, none lost; the three Myrmecia species shared with Herberstein_etal_2022 -- nigriceps, nigrocincta, tarsata -- now average two sources, log10_range <= 0.11); `TaxonBodyMass_GenusLevel.csv` 10,725 -> 10,741 (+16 ant genera; Aphaenogaster, Camponotus, Crematogaster, Myrmecia, Pheidole, Tetramorium re-averaged); `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 121,417 rows for 38,616 species, 53 Leahy_2025 rows all `measured_in_source` / `self`; 314 distinct primary CiteIDs; `reports/warnings_citations.md`: Leahy_2025 has no Bibcite / CiteID row yet (as Hudson_2013). Three names unresolved by every enrichment stage and dropped (Chelaner cinctum 89 rows, Ochetellus clarithorax 10, Technomyrmex nitida 6; GBIF knows Chelaner and Technomyrmex at genus rank only and O. clarithorax only as the subspecies Ochetellus glaber clarithorax); Myrmecia pyriformis (13 Leahy and 18 Herberstein records) is lost to the autotroph filter because its cached NCBI resolution reads kingdom Viridiplantae (the algal homonym Myrmecia) -- both put to the owner. No dependency edge: no source in the database copies these measurements.
- Still open for Leahy_2025: the `BM_citations` Sheet row and curated bib entry for `Leahy:2025aa` / `Leahy_2025` (owner); the three unresolved names and the Myrmecia pyriformis kingdom (owner decisions, see the hand-back of 2026-10-04).
## 2026-10-04 -- Wascher_2025 (CORVIDATA, new source, issue #78; Phases A and B)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-78-wascher-2025`, merged with main fe128d1 after the Chown and Brose rounds).

- Source: Wascher (2025) Scientific Data 12:2032, Zenodo 10.5281/zenodo.17531251 (CC BY 4.0), `CORVIDATA.xlsx`; 135 corvid species, 129 with a species-average body mass, every value copied from a reference named in `bodymass_ref` (AVONET 122, HBW vol. 14 4, BIRDBASE 2, Atwood 1980 1). The parser reads the workbook, not the CSV export of the tab, which rounds AVONET's two-decimal masses to one decimal; it drops the five AVONET-cited values that AVONET itself flags Inferred (Body Mass) or EltonTraits_Model (`DropImputed()`, as the Tobias_2022 parser does) and keeps the reference key of every kept record as `ref_keys` (124 records, 4 keys, 0 NA).
- Registry: `Wascher_2025` (compilation, unknown); edges `Wascher_2025 -> Tobias_2022` (copies, 1e-6, priority 10: 120 of the 122 AVONET-cited masses equal the `AVONET1_BirdLife` row, Corvus minutus and Pica nuttalli the `AVONET3_BirdTree` row), `-> delHoyo_2009` and `-> Sekercioglu_2025` (external, provenance). Full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off): +1 species (Pica serica, 208.75 g, BIRDBASE, single source), genus-level file unchanged, 122 species gain the label, 11 species' means move (at most 0.049 log10: Aphelocoma woodhouseii 82.75 -> 73.88 g with the BIRDBASE 65 g as a second independent value); 119 of the 123 Wascher values collapse as copies (117 into Tobias_2022).
- `--init --verify --queue` (live Crossref + OpenAlex, 4 references): certain 2 (`Tobias_2022` 10.1111/ele.13898, `Sekercioglu_2025` 10.1038/s41597-025-05615-3, two-service agreement), not_found 2 (`delHoyo_2009` HBW vol. 14, best candidate a book review at 0.636; `Atwood_1980` North American Bird Bander note, best candidate 0.407); 2 rows queued.
- Screening: Scite not called (quota exhausted until 2026-11-01); Consensus confirmed both certain DOIs by title (`Bib/scite_checks.csv`, `consensus-mcp`, `unchecked`); Consensus knows Atwood's 1980 breeding-biology paper but not the body-weight note. Crossref `update-to` / OpenAlex `is_retracted`: clean. **Scite re-screening due after 2026-11-01.**
- Hop 2 not yet active: the AVONET reference has no `cite_id` / `bibcite` before `--bib`, and the curated `Tobias:2022aa` has no `doi` field, so `MatchIntermediateLabel()` cannot recognise it; the 117 AVONET records read `compiled_from` in `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` (121,815 rows for 38,567 species; Wascher_2025 118 of 123 record links resolved) until the owner adds the DOI to `Tobias:2022aa` (the `Chown:2007aa` precedent) and `--bib` runs.
- Owner decisions (2026-10-04, recorded in `Bib/pending_citations.csv` and the `parsed_*` / `role` / `notes` of `primary_references.csv`; `--apply-queue` / `--bib` / `--sheet` not yet run, Phase C sequenced by the coordinator): `delHoyo_2009` -> `nodoi` (del Hoyo, J. and Elliott, A. and Christie, D. A.; Handbook of the Birds of the World, vol. 14: Bush-shrikes to Old World Sparrows; Lynx Edicions, Barcelona; 2009; role `compilation` -> `compilation_terminal`, as the HBW volumes in Wilman_etal_2014); `Atwood_1980` -> `nodoi` from the fields as printed (volume 4, no. 4, page 2; the inconsistency with the journal numbering noted, not corrected). The Zenodo workbook stays the data of record, the owner's rounded CSV export stays untracked. Corvus hawaiiensis kept (tracked in the new issue "Unresolved or re-placed taxon names"). Owner-accepted bib edits: `doi = {10.1111/ele.13898}` added to `Tobias:2022aa` in `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_Citations.bib` (the source `Citation.bib` already carried it) so that hop 2 to Tobias_2022 fires; `Wascher:2025aa` copied into the curated bib (the `BM_citations` Sheet row is the owner's).
- Phase C (2026-10-04, coordinator's go): `--apply-queue --bib`: delHoyo_2009 and Atwood_1980 -> `nodoi_approved`; 390 entries in `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_PrimaryCitations.bib` (5 reuse a curated key), Wascher_2025 4 rows with bibcite (`Tobias:2022aa` reused through the DOI, `Sekercioglu:2025aa`, `del-Hoyo:2009aa` (@book), `Atwood:1980aa`). `--sheet` dry run then real: 4 rows appended to `BM_primary_citations` (391 -> 395), snapshots updated. The branch then took main 3a214a2 (Lemoine_2026 #87, Leahy_2025 #82; `--bib` rebuilt on the merged tree, same 390 entries) and the full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off) gave: Wascher_2025 123 of 123 record links resolved -- 116 `compilation_terminal` at Tobias_2022 through hop 2 (117 AVONET records minus Cyanolyca cucullata, range filter), 4 `compilation_terminal` at delHoyo_2009, 2 `compiled_from` Sekercioglu_2025, 1 `compiled_from` Atwood_1980; provenance table 125,247 rows for 38,525 species; `reports/warnings_citations.md`: Wascher_2025 100% resolved. Against main 3a214a2: +1 species (Pica serica, 38,524 -> 38,525), genus-level file unchanged (10,749), 122 species gain the label, 11 means move by at most 0.049 log10, 119 of the 123 Wascher values collapse as copies. Remaining owner item: the `BM_citations` Sheet row for `Wascher_2025` (`\citep{Wascher:2025aa}`); Corvus hawaiiensis is tracked in issue #85.


## 2026-10-04 -- Wisnionski_2026 (new source, issue #79; tier A2 of issue #1)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-79-wisnionski-2026`, from main edcffcf).

- Source: Wisnionski et al. (2026) Ecol. Evol. 16:e74243 (CC BY 4.0), Dryad 10.5061/dryad.79cnp5jbt; the three class sheets of `VertData.xlsx` as csv (48 reptiles, 82 birds, 34 mammals), masses in g, one row per species, no reference column. The Wiley supplement (`ece374243-sup-0001-supinfo.pdf`, the owner's PDF rendering of the Word file) prints Table S1 with a bracketed reference number after every body mass and the numbered list 1-132; `parse_wisnionski_supmat.py` (PyMuPDF, fixed column bands, wrapped brackets re-joined) writes `TableS1_parsed.csv` and `references.csv` (132 verbatim entries, 8 without DOI).
- Parser: `BodyMass_Wisnionski_2026.r` matches every data-file row to Table S1 (24 name differences mapped; masses must agree within 0.5 g / 0.5 %), keeps the mass reference number as `ref_keys` (157 records, 0 NA, 53 keys), drops 2 rows without a mass and 5 juvenile / young-of-year rows (`DropImputed()`), truncates 5 trinomials; the two black-browed albatross rows keep 56 (Dunning 1993, 3,564 g = the McCoy_2008 Dunning value) and 102 (Angelier et al. 2007, 3,540 g). Classes Reptilia 42, Aves 81, Mammalia 34.
- Dependency edges (`Bib/source_dependencies.csv`): `-> AnAge` (reference 4, 41 rows; 31 shared with the Build 13 frame, 24 identical, 29 within 0.0105 after rounding to the gram), `-> Dunning_2008` (reference 56 = Dunning 1993, 25 rows; McCoy_2008 shared 13, identical 5, within 0.0105 11; Tobias_2022 identical 2 of 25), `-> Bokony_2009` (reference 68, 27 rows), `-> Gillooly_2017` (reference 7, 4 rows), `-> White_2006` (reference 40, 2 rows). The register's `provenance` row: compilation, default unknown.
- `--init --verify --queue` (live Crossref + OpenAlex with the owner's API key, cached; the compilation's `Citation.bib` carries no DOI, so no closed-world candidates although Crossref deposits 79 references for 10.1002/ece3.74243): certain 48 (doi_resolves 46, two_service_agreement 2), pending 1 (56 Dunning 1993, ambiguous: the 2007/2008 CRC edition DOI 10.1201/9781420064452 and a 1994 review of the first edition), not_found 4 (below_threshold: 14 Barns 2015 MTSU thesis, 25 Schuett et al. 2004 Herpetological Review, 122 Dyavolova et al. 2014 symposium proceedings, 124 Kotwica et al. 2004 Reproductive Biology); 5 rows queued. Two year discrepancies in the source's list (6 Summers & Norman printed 2005, the DOI is the 1988 J. Exp. Zool. article; 4 de Magalhaes et al. printed 2024 for the NAR 2024 database issue published online 2023) are carried by the Crossref record.
- Screening: Scite MCP not called (quota exhausted until 2026-11-01); Consensus confirmed all 48 certain DOIs and the Dunning 2007 candidate by title, first author, year and journal (49 rows in `Bib/scite_checks.csv`, `consensus-mcp`, `unchecked`; `--verify` re-run from the cache so `services` records the screen). Crossref `update-to` / OpenAlex `is_retracted`: no notice on any of the 48. **Scite re-screening due after 2026-11-01.**
- Full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off): species 38,566 before and after (Crotalus helleri enters as a species of its own; Crocodylus johnsoni leaves because the source's 141,832 g pushes its log10 range over 1), genus table 10,726 rows unchanged; 131 species rows carry the label (the other 26 kept records are species the range filter already excluded); `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 121,456 rows, `compiled_from` 1,985 -> 2,116; `reports/warnings_citations.md`: 19 references pending / not found overall (5 of them Wisnionski's), labels without a Bibcite: Wisnionski_2026 (new; Sheet row pending) and Hudson_2013 (pre-existing). Two csv misspellings (Thallasarche melanophris, Thryesphilus rufalbus) added to `fix_misspellings.r`.
- Owner decisions (2026-10-04, `Bib/pending_citations.csv`): 56 -> `nodoi` as a separate first-edition book entry (`Dunning:1993aa`, Dunning, J. B., CRC Press, Boca Raton, 1993; not the 2008 DOI); 14, 25, 122, 124 -> `nodoi` from the stated fields (author lists in BibTeX form corrected in `primary_references.csv`, as in earlier rounds; the Barns MTSU Master's thesis comes out `@phdthesis` because `BuildBibEntryNoDOI()` has no Master's type -- tooling follow-up, not extended here). Crocodylus johnstoni 141,832 g kept and registered CRITICAL in `audit/flagged_species.csv` (`source_conflict_2026-10-04`); the csv names Crotalus helleri and Thamnophis elegans kept (tracked in the owner's taxon-name issue); domestic forms and the Mix-stage Sturnus row kept; `doi` added to `Citation.bib` and the entry copied to the curated bib.
- `--apply-queue --bib`: 5 decisions applied; 443 entries in `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_PrimaryCitations.bib` (RefManageR parses all; 5 reuse a curated key), Wisnionski_2026 53 rows with bibcite. `--sheet` dry run, then real: 53 rows appended to `BM_primary_citations` (395 -> 448). `--bib` also rewrote two other sources' `primary_references.csv` (Leahy_2025 header quoting only; Wilman_etal_2014 Dunning08 `cite_id` Dunning_2008 -> DunningJr_2007, re-derived by DOI from the Sheet row `DunningJr_2007` / `Dunning-Jr:2007aa` that predates the key's rename): both restored to main's state, the Dunning08 Sheet row remains the owner's pending fix.
- Full run on main a0bb052 (recompile = TRUE, downloads off): species 38,741 before and after (Crotalus helleri in, Crocodylus johnsoni out by the range filter), 51 species' masses move where the label joins, genus table 10,793 rows unchanged; 131 species rows carry the label; provenance 125,730 rows, Wisnionski_2026 131 species / 131 record links, 100 % resolved (53 references, hop 1); 426 distinct primary CiteIDs; `reports/warnings_citations.md`: the labels without a Bibcite (Wisnionski_2026 and five earlier sources awaiting their `BM_citations` Sheet row) and the `Dunning-Jr:2007aa` Sheet row predate or await the owner.

Bohemoslov. 8:250-259, 1 record; Speakman & Thomas 2003, chapter in Kunz & Fenton (eds) Bat Ecology, 1 record), unverified 1 ('McNab 1989', Myotis nigricans 3.7 g, 1 record: a key in neither of the supplement's reference lists, which hold McNab 1969 and McNab 1980). 2 rows queued. Hanus 1959 is not in Consensus (only a 1958 Czech note by K. Hanus); the Speakman & Thomas 2003 chapter is not indexed (only a review of the book). ## 2026-10-04 -- Baach_2026 (new source, issue #72; Phases A and B)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-72-baach-2026`, from main edcffcf).

Edges registered in `Bib/source_dependencies.csv`: `Baach_2026 -> Chown_etal_2007` (copies, 1e-6; 622 rows, species shared 258, identical 258), `-> Wilman_etal_2014` (copies, 1e-6; 108 rows, 62 bird species shared, 62 identical; the EltonTraits mammals await #65), `-> Tobias_2022` (copies, 0.0105; 80 rows re-rounded to one decimal, 79 shared, 79 within tolerance).

- `--init --verify --queue` (live Crossref + OpenAlex with the owner's key; the compilation's own Crossref record is not in the curated bib, so no closed-world candidates): certain 4 (AVONET 10.1111/ele.13898, EltonTraits 10.1890/13-1917.1 and Rendon et al. 2019 10.1371/journal.pone.0210296 `doi_resolves` from the sheet's doi.org links; Chown et al. 2007 10.1111/j.1365-2435.2007.01245.x `two_service_agreement` from the JSTOR-linked entry), pending 2 (Handbuch der Vogel Mitteleuropas `weak_match` 0.78 against a 1972 J. Wildl. ## 2026-10-04 -- Oskyrko_2024 (ReptTraits; tier A2, issue #77; Phases A and B, no PR)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-77-oskyrko-2024` from main edcffcf).

- Source: ReptTraits v1.2 (Oskyrko, Mi, Meiri & Du 2024, Scientific Data 11:243; figshare 10.6084/m9.figshare.24572683, CC BY 4.0), the four sheets as csv. Trait T20 `Maximum body mass (g)` holds 10,469 species maxima; the paper's Methods say "most mass data are based on lengths, transformed to masses based on taxon-specific equations", and the values confirm it: 5,957 are identical to the Feldman_etal_2016 or Meiri_2018 SVL-derived value of the species and 8,937 (85%) are within 0.0105 log10 of it. The sources sheet cites the length's source, not a weighed animal.
- Parser: `BodyMass_Oskyrko_2024.r` drops, through `DropImputed()`, 9,765 rows cited to the length-mass allometry compilations (Slavenko et al. 2019, Zimin et al. 2022, Meiri et al. 2021 -- "we converted lengths to masses using the allometric equations published by Feldman et al.", maxima -- and the Feldman works), 619 rows whose value is identical to the Feldman_etal_2016 / Meiri_2018 value of the species and 29 within 0.0105 of it; 56 records remain (40 squamates, 15 turtles, 1 crocodilian), none demonstrably measured (owner decision pending, see the README). The citation cell of every kept row is `ref_keys` (`SplitRefKeys(adat$mass_src, ',')`; 56 of 56 keyed, 22 keys). Registry: `Oskyrko_2024 -> Feldman_etal_2016` (copies, 0.001) and `Oskyrko_2024 -> Meiri_2018` (derived_same_input, 0.001), documenting the removed copies; at accepted-name level the kept frame still shares 25 species with Feldman_etal_2016, 16 identical (Feldman values under synonyms, collapsed by the edge: `reports/dedupe_summary.md`).
- Pipeline (recompile, downloads off): 38,566 -> 38,581 species (+17 new, -2 removed by the range filter because of an Oskyrko value: Philodryas boliviana 1,245.6 vs 66.3 g Feldman; Chironius quadricarinatus, under its ReptTraits synonym Phrynonax sexcarinatus, 681.3 vs 61 g Makarieva), 10,725 -> 10,728 genera; 52 species rows carry the label (Zonateres lanei unresolved by every enrichment stage; Alligator mississippiensis already removed by the range filter before this source).
- Reference list: `references.csv` (`key`, `citation`) transcribed from `ReptTraits dataset v1-2_references.csv` for the 22 keys of the kept records: 19 verbatim entries (three matched by the species' family where the list holds two entries of the author and year: Kraus 2017a, Shine et al. 2006 -> 2006a, Wallach & Gemel 2018), 3 keys absent from the list (Aresco 2009; Wilson and Heinsohn 2007; Wilson et al. 2006b).
- Tooling fix found on this round: `ParseCitationString()` took the first year in the string, so the Nature / Scientific Data style ("Authors. Title. Container vol(issue), pages, (year).") parsed to an empty title (or to a page number, "1770-74") and every reference scored `below_threshold` although Crossref's first candidate was the right work. A bracketed year that closes the entry is now the year, and the author block ends at the first title word (`SplitAuthorsFromTitle()`, `normalise_citation.r`; five new parse tests). The first `--init --verify --queue` pass (19 `below_threshold` rows) was discarded before anything was committed: `Bib/pending_citations.csv` reverted to HEAD, `primary_references.csv` and the report deleted, and the pass run once more with the fixed parser.
- `--init --verify --queue` (no compilation DOI deposit list; OpenAlex with the owner's key, ~40 requests, 23 s): certain 9 (two_service_agreement), pending 7 (ambiguous 2: O'Shea 2018 -- the 2018 and 2023 Chicago editions of the same book --, Spawls et al. 2018; weak_match 5: Iverson et al. 1993, van Leenders 2019, Seymor 1987, Carey and Judge 2000, Vitt & Valdinger 1983 -- the keys as written in the sources sheet; the list misspells Seymour and Vangilder), not_found 3 (below_threshold: Wallach & Gemel 2018 Herpetozoa, Labanowski & Lowin 2011 Herpetology Notes, Lobo et al. 2004 Hamadryad -- journals without Crossref DOIs for those years), unverified 3 (keys not in the list); 10 rows queued.
- Screening: Scite not called (monthly MCP quota exhausted until 2026-11-01); Consensus confirmed the 9 certain DOIs by title (`Bib/scite_checks.csv`, `consensus-mcp`, `unchecked`); Crossref `update-to` and OpenAlex `is_retracted` clean. **Scite re-screening due after 2026-11-01.** Zimin et al. 2022 (a dropped citation, not verified) carries a 2024 correction unrelated to body mass.
- Second full run: species and genus tables unchanged from the first; `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 121,381 rows for 38,581 species (compiled_from 1,985 -> 2,043); Oskyrko_2024 coverage: 52 species, 58 record links, 22 keyed; `reports/warnings_citations.md` lists the label without a Bibcite (no `BM_citations` row yet) and the 9 accepted references without a bib entry (`--bib` not run: Phase C).
- Owner decisions (2026-10-04): option A2 -- the parser's fourth `DropImputed()` step removes the 25 kept rows whose species has a Feldman_etal_2016 / Meiri_2018 value at accepted-name level (raw name, gender ending or one of eleven listed genus transfers), 31 records remain (15 squamates, 15 turtles, 1 crocodilian); after the merge with main 8b50313 the run gives 38,422 -> 38,439 species (+17, none removed; Philodryas boliviana and Chironius quadricarinatus keep their main values), 10,692 -> 10,695 genera, 29 species rows with the label, both Oskyrko edges shared 0 in `reports/dedupe_summary.md` (7 turtle values blind-collapsed into Myhrvold_2015, 5 turtle means move). Queue: `1` for O'Shea 2018 (2018 edition), Iverson et al. 1993, van Leenders 2019, Seymor 1987, Vitt & Valdinger 1983; `nodoi` for Carey and Judge 2000, Spawls et al. 2018, Wallach & Gemel 2018, Labanowski & Lowin 2011, Lobo et al. 2004 (decided_by owner; five of these keys are no longer cited after A2, the decisions stay recorded); the three keys absent from the ReptTraits list -> `drop` (cite the compilation), recorded in the `notes` of `primary_references.csv` since an unverified key has no queue row. `Oskyrko:2024aa` copied into `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_Citations.bib` (the BM_citations row is the owner's). Phase C (2026-10-05, owner's go; merged with main 62cfbeb, Baach PR #93): `--apply-queue --bib` applied the 10 decisions (approved: O'Shea 2018, Iverson et al. 1993, van Leenders 2019, Seymor 1987, Vitt & Valdinger 1983; nodoi_approved: Carey and Judge 2000, Spawls et al. 2018, Wallach & Gemel 2018, Labanowski & Lowin 2011, Lobo et al. 2004) and wrote 19 entries to `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_PrimaryCitations.bib` (465 -> 484, RefManageR parses all); `--bib` also rewrote `Leahy_2025/primary_references.csv` and `Wilman_etal_2014/primary_references.csv` (the stale `DunningJr_2007` snapshot row: Wilman's Dunning08 cite_id and Leahy's formatting), both restored to main's state with `git checkout HEAD --`. `--sheet` dry run then real: 19 rows appended to `BM_primary_citations` (tab 471 -> 490; snapshot updated). Full run (recompile, downloads off): 38,752 -> 38,769 species (+17, none removed), 10,799 -> 10,802 genera, 29 species rows with the label, both Oskyrko edges shared 0; coverage 30 of 33 record links (90.9%) with a primary CiteID (certain 11, approved 15, nodoi 4; the 3 links of the dropped keys `unmatched_key`), 19 distinct primary CiteIDs; the 5 turtle means shared with Amniote move as before. Still open: the `BM_citations` Sheet row for `Oskyrko_2024`; Scite re-screen after 2026-11-01. Merge note: main's `ParseNatureStyle()` (Wilman round) and this branch's bracketed-year branch of `ParseCitationString()` both kept, main's first; the ReptTraits punctuation ("71 (12), 2448-61, (1993).", "Press, (2018).") is covered only by the latter.

## 2026-10-04 -- Gonzalez_2024 (new source, issue #74; Phases A and B)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-74-gonzalez-2024`, rebased on main 5637575 after the Chown round).

- Source: StoichLife (González et al. 2025, Scientific Data 12:569; Dryad 10.5061/dryad.3tx95x6r2, CC0). Parser `BodyMass_Gonzalez_2024.r`: species-level animal rows with a body dry mass, converted to wet grams by class / phylum / habitat with the `mass_conversion.r` groups; 3,576 records of 336 species from 50 `data.source1` keys, kept as `ref_keys` (0 NA). Dropped through `DropImputed()`: freshwater Odonata / Trichoptera nymphs and the bromeliad mosquito larvae of Gonzalez et al. 2018 (183 rows), the Roussel salmonids (212) and Harrower ants (36) with unverifiable units, and the Amphibia (534 rows, 9 species: larvae, tadpoles and metamorphs). Reference list `references.csv` built from the Dryad sheet References by `build_references.r` (111 keys; the 22 Vanni-database sub-sources and `Urabe 1993` are not in the sheet and keep their short key as the citation; the `unpublished - <contributor>` template rows are `self`).
- `--init --verify --queue` (live Crossref + OpenAlex with the owner's key, cached; the compilation's Crossref record deposits no references): certain 22 (`doi_resolves`), self 13, pending 3 (doi_mismatch Goncharov et al. 2016 -- the sheet gives Rizzuto's bioRxiv DOI 10.1101/594101 -- and Rizzuto et al. 2019 -- the sheet's `10.1101/594101v1` does not resolve; grey_literature `Dalton, CM & AS Flecker, unpublished`), not_found 12 (the Vanni-database sub-sources `Vanni et al 2002`, `McIntyre et al 2008`, `Torres & Vanni 2007`, `Small et al 2011`, `McManamay et al 2011`, `Sereda et al 2008`, `Johnson et al 2010`, `Sterrett et al 2015`, `Taylor et al. 2012`, `Tarvainen et al 2005`, `Zimmer et al 2006`, `McIntyre, PB, unpub`: short keys without a title); 16 rows queued. The first `--init` ran before the rebase, with the pre-#63 parser, which read no title from the Nature-style entries (Benstead et al. 2010 pending at title_sim 0.19); after the rebase the file was re-initialised from scratch (`--init --verify`, no second `--queue`): Benstead is certain (title_sim 1.0) and its queue row is moot. Tooling finding: `doi_resolves` accepts a DOI on author and year alone, so `González et al. 2011` is certain although the sheet's DOI (10.1007/s00442-011-2032-y, Oecologia) is another González et al. 2011 paper; the article the sheet describes (Oikos 120: 1247-1255) is 10.1111/j.1600-0706.2010.19151.x at Crossref (decision table of the hand-back).
- Screening: Scite MCP quota exhausted until 2026-11-01 (not called); Consensus confirmed by title, author, year and journal 21 of the 22 certain DOIs (not the Oecologia DOI above) and the candidate articles Torres & Vanni 2007 (Oikos 116; two Wiley DOIs resolve to it), Goncharov et al. 2016 (Ecol. Res. 31, 10.1007/s11284-016-1402-7), Rizzuto et al. 2019 (Ecol. Evol. 9, 10.1002/ece3.5880), González et al. 2011 (Oikos), McIntyre et al. 2008 (Ecology 89, 10.1890/07-1552.1), Vanni et al. 2002 (Ecol. Lett. 5, 10.1046/j.1461-0248.2002.00314.x), McManamay et al. 2011 (JNABS 30, 10.1899/09-152.1), Sereda et al. 2008 (Freshw. Biol. 53, 10.1111/j.1365-2427.2008.02029.x) and Sterrett et al. 2015 (Freshw. Biol. 60, 10.1111/fwb.12516); Small et al. 2011 (Ecology 92, 10.1890/10-0081.1) was found at Crossref only. 28 rows added to `Bib/scite_checks.csv` (`consensus-mcp`, `unchecked`; Tande 1982 and Ikeda & Skjoldal 1989 were already screened for Kiorboe_2013). Crossref `update-to` / OpenAlex `is_retracted`: no notice. **Scite re-screening due after 2026-11-01.**
- Owner decisions: none yet (no `--apply-queue`, `--bib` or `--sheet`); the decision table is in the hand-back of issue #74.

## 2026-10-05 -- Vanni_2017 (new source, issue #99; Phases A and B)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-99-vanni-2017` from main adbc37a).

- Source: Vanni et al. (2017) A global database of nitrogen and phosphorus excretion rates of aquatic animals, Ecology 98:1475 (10.1002/ecy.1792; Wiley Supporting Information, owner's download of 2026-10-05; the data csv files committed under the authors' use statement of Metadata S1, the publisher PDF kept untracked). Parser `BodyMass_Vanni_2017.r`: rows named with a clean binomial (after bracket, trinomial and generation clean-ups in the parser), dry mass converted to wet grams with the `mass_conversion.r` groups (the vertebrate classes with the generic 0.25, the inverse of the compilers' own DM = 0.25 WM; molluscs scaled to whole mass with the Brey shell ratios, pelagic pteropods excepted); 3,698 records of 163 species from 45 `Source name` keys, kept as `ref_keys` (0 NA). Dropped through `DropImputed()`: 205 stage-named rows (euphausiid calyptopis / furcilia, megalopa, nauplii, veliger, insect larvae, the 1.7 mg *Ranzania laevis* larva), 111 Insecta (aquatic larvae and nymphs), 417 Amphibia (tadpoles and larval salamanders, 1.6-1.9 log10 below the adult values) and, as option (b) of the fish-size question put to the owner, 3,642 Osteichthyes of 137 species (species x study means a median 0.81 log10 below the database's values over 60 pairs, 0.3-1.9 by study; the fish exclusion is one toggled call). Reference list `references.csv` from the 'Data source references' of Metadata S1 (`build_references.py`, pdftotext; 84 verbatim entries, the 90 `Source name` strings as keys, 8 keys without an entry keep the key as citation). Label registered (`compilation`, default `unknown`); `Vanni:2017aa` copied into `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_Citations.bib` with the Crossref DOI (the BM_citations Sheet row is the owner's).
- `--init --verify --queue` (live Crossref + OpenAlex with the owner's key; the compilation's Crossref record deposits no references): certain 32 (`two_service_agreement`), self 1 ('Vanni, MJ unpubl'), pending 9 (`ambiguous`: Arnott & Vanni 1996, Wilhelm et al. 1999, Hall et al. 2003, James et al. 2000 -- two DOIs for one article, the CJFAS `f`-form, the Frontiers `1540-9295` form and the journal article over the DTIC report are the candidates to approve -- and 'Ikeda database', which matches Ikeda 1985 Mar. Biol. 85:1-11; `weak_match`: Andersen 1989, Bamstedt & Tande 1985, Paffenhofer & Gardner 1984, Srna & Baggaley 1976, each with the right article as candidate 1 at a lowered title or author agreement), not_found 3 ('McIntyre, PB, unpub', 'Gauvin et al 1989', 'Nalepa et al 1991'); 12 rows queued. A first `--init` stopped on the `;`-separated references.csv before writing anything (the csv reader takes `csv_sep`, the spec's `sep` is the ref-key separator); the file was rewritten comma-separated and the command run once.
- Screening: Scite MCP quota exhausted until 2026-11-01 (not called); Consensus confirmed by title, author, year and journal all 32 certain DOIs (28 screened in this round, 4 -- Benstead et al. 2010, Christian et al. 2008, Follum & Gray 1987, Sterrett et al. 2015 -- in the Gonzalez_2025 round; Johnson et al. 2010 is listed by Consensus under the coauthor Luecke, Hall et al. 2007 as the Cambridge chapter without journal); 28 rows added to `Bib/scite_checks.csv` (`consensus-mcp`, `unchecked`); `--verify --force` marked the 32 rows `;consensus-mcp`. Crossref `update-to` / OpenAlex `is_retracted`: no notice. **Scite re-screening due after 2026-11-01.**

- Dependencies: `Vanni_2017 -> Ikeda_2014` registered `provenance_only` (the 'Ikeda database' rows, 1,297 kept, are Ikeda's individual measurements; frames shared 75, exact 2; run: shared 90, 4 blind collapses) and `Gonzalez_2025 -> Vanni_2017` registered `copies` at 0.001 (StoichLife's `Vanni_database` rows are verbatim copies of records of this file, the 38 rows kept by both parsers -- Sterrett et al. 2015 turtles, one Johnson et al. 2010 copepod, five 'McIntyre, PB, unpub' snails -- each a subset of the parent's record set, so only *Heterocope septentrionalis* agrees at species level; run: shared 49, exact 8, within tolerance 11, 11 Gonzalez values collapsed into Vanni_2017, mostly the Ikeda-database and Benstead *Atya* species both copy from the same primaries).

## 2026-10-05 -- Ghaderi_2026 (issue #73; Phase B: the article PDF checked for the 85 description references, no ingestion)

Branch `issue-73-ghaderi-references` from main adbc37a; no tooling run (nothing to `--init`).

- The owner placed the article PDF (Ghaderi, Nielsen & He 2026, Applied Soil Ecology 217:106605; 7 pages, Elsevier, CC BY) in the source folder of the main checkout, untracked; read with `pdftotext -layout` into /tmp and not committed. Its reference list holds the same 30 entries the Crossref record deposits (Ahmad & Jairajpuri 2010; Andrassy 1956, 2009; Angst et al. 2024; Bardgett & van der Putten 2014; Bonfanti et al. 2024; Chapin et al. 2011; de Bello et al. 2021; du Preez et al. 2022; Elbrecht & Leese 2015; Ferris 2010; Ferris & Benavides 2024; Ghaderi 2020; Ghaderi et al. 2025; Jager et al. 2005; Joimel et al. 2024; Kooijman 2000; Kopittke et al. 2022; Maciute et al. 2021; Mulder et al. 2005; Mulder & Maas 2017; Procter 1987; Schenk et al. 2019; Tu et al. 2024; van den Hoogen et al. 2019; Whalen et al. 2013; Ye & Hunt 2021; Zhang et al. 2024a, 2024b; Zhao et al. 2019); no appendix or supplementary reference list. Of the workbook's 85 `Source` keys (559 adult records) only the two book keys `Ahmad & Jairajpuri, 2010` (92 records) and `Andrassy, 2009` (47) appear in it; the 83 journal keys (420 records, 75%) do not. The Methods say the body sizes were "compiled from publicly available literature including reference books (Andrassy, 2009; Ahmad and Jairajpuri, 2010) and peer-reviewed papers published in international journals", the Data availability statement names only the Supplementary Data (the committed workbook), so the article points to no further list.
- Decision taken (the branch's conditional instruction: build the list only if most keys resolve): no `references.csv`, no `reflist_specs` entry, no `primary_references.csv`; the source stays `uningested` (355 provenance rows). Options for the owner are in the hand-back: (a) ask the authors (corresponding author J.-Z. He) for the reference list behind the `Source` column; (b) ingest the two book keys now (Ahmad & Jairajpuri 2010 is the Brill monograph 10.1163/ej.9789004174641.i-298, Crossref type `monograph`, title deposited as "Mononchida" -- the series title reads "Mononchida: The Predatory Soil Nematodes", Nematology Monographs and Perspectives 7; Andrassy 2009 has no Crossref record, only a DataCite deposit 10.5281/zenodo.10821935 of pages 382-415, hence `nodoi` by the standing policy), which would flip the other 420 records to `unmatched_key`; (c) reconstruct the 83 citations from the author-year keys and the species names (most are descriptions in Nematology, Zootaxa, Journal of Nematology, Nematologica, Nematologia Mediterranea, Indian Journal of Nematology and the like, many older ones without DOIs), to be verified like any other list. Key spelling variants to reconcile then: `Coetze, 1966` / `Coetzee, 1966`; `de Braun & Heyns, 1992` / `de Bruin & Heyns, 1992`; `Mulvey & Jansen, 1967` / `Mulvey & Jensen, 1967`; `Jansen & Mulvey, 1968`; `Orselli & Vincicuerra, 2007` / `Vinceguerra & Orcelli, 2006`; `Ishaq et al., 2021` / `Ishaque et al., 2022`.
- Equation reference (registry TODO, not applied): the PDF prints "Andrássy, I., 1956. Die rauminhalst and gewichtsbestimmung der fadenwurmer, (Nematoden). Acta Zool. Acad. Sci. Hung. 2, 1–15." (the correct title is "Die Rauminhalts- und Gewichtsbestimmung der Fadenwürmer (Nematoden)", Acta Zoologica Academiae Scientiarum Hungaricae 2:1-15). Crossref returns no record for it (author/year and title-word queries); Consensus returns only a DOI-less "The determination of volume and weight of nematodes" record dated 1967. Proposed for the owner: a `nodoi` entry with role `equation` (`Andrassy:1956aa`, @article, author Andrássy, István; year 1956; journal Acta Zoologica Academiae Scientiarum Hungaricae; volume 2; pages 1-15) as `equation_bibcite` of `Ghaderi_2026` (and of `Mulder_2011`'s README, which cites the same formula).
- Screening: Scite not called (quota until 2026-11-01, and no accepted DOI); Consensus consulted for the three works above (no rows appended to `Bib/scite_checks.csv`: nothing was verified by the tool).
- The owner's `Ghaderi_2017-Gindex.csv` (untracked, main checkout) is the `G index` sheet of the committed workbook saved as CSV at display precision (same 20 columns and 98 rows plus 4 empty trailing rows; `D`, `G%`, the masses and the carbon columns rounded to the sheet's number formats, e.g. `G%` 12.35 -> 12.4) and holds nothing the workbook lacks: redundant, to stay untracked.
- Pipeline not re-run (no `primary_references.csv` changed): `TaxonBodyMass.csv` and `TaxonBodyMass_GenusLevel.csv` are those of main adbc37a.

## 2026-10-05 -- Cited bird and mammal dry-to-wet conversion groups (issue #86)

- Follow-up of the Gonzalez_2025 round: 119 StoichLife records (14 birds of 5 species, 50 *Lepus americanus*, 55 *Liolaemus* / *Phyllodactylus* lizards) were converted with the generic `vertebrate` factor (0.25, no conversion CiteID). Branch `issue-86-vertebrate-conversion`; plan comment https://github.com/TaxonBodyMassML/TaxonBodyMass_DB/issues/86#issuecomment-6002863909.
- Horn & de la Vega (2016, J. Exp. Mar. Biol. Ecol. 481:41-48) is closed access (Scite quota exhausted until 2026-11-01, Consensus gave the abstract: carbon : fresh weight 0.16-0.22 for the birds, 0.17 for the seals), but the authors deposited the raw data in PANGAEA under CC BY 3.0 in supplement to the article: 894380 (17 Wadden Sea birds of 6 species: whole-bird wet mass, three subsamples each with wet, dry, ash and ash-free dry mass, %C and %N) and 894400 (tissue samples of 3 harbour seals, no whole-body value). Stored in `sources/conversion_factors/Horn_2016/` with `summarise_pangaea.py` -> `horn2016_species_ratios.csv`: species means of DW/WW 0.3805 (*Larus canus*) to 0.4340 (*Haematopus ostralegus*); species-level medians DW/WW 0.3906, AFDW/DW 0.8527, C/WW 0.1677 = the new `bird` group (`Horn:2016aa`, CiteID `Horn_2016`, verified). StoichLife's `body.weight.gr` for these birds is the wet mass x the bird's own DW/WW rounded to two decimals, so the factor restores the measured wet masses.
- Rizzuto et al. (2019, Ecol. Evol. 9:14453-14464; figshare 10.6084/m9.figshare.7884854, CC BY 4.0) measured the 50 snowshoe hares of StoichLife: whole-hare wet mass and the wet and dry mass of the carcass homogenate; StoichLife's hare "dry mass" is the wet mass x that ratio (45 of 50 rows to 0.05 g). Stored in `sources/conversion_factors/Rizzuto_2019/` with `summarise_hares.py` -> `rizzuto2019_hare_ratios.csv`: DW/WW median 0.2925 (mean 0.2899, range 0.222-0.357), C/WW 0.1290 = the new `mammal` group (`Rizzuto:2019aa`, CiteID `Rizzuto_2019`, verified; a one-species basis, stated in the module). The lizards stay on `vertebrate` (no citable reptile factor verified; Thorson 1968 closed access); Vanni_2017's fishes and turtles stay on `vertebrate` because 0.25 is the exact inverse of the compilers' DM = 0.25 WM. No seal group.
- Bibliography: `Horn:2016aa` and `Rizzuto:2019aa` moved from the generated `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_PrimaryCitations.bib` to the curated `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_Citations.bib` (entries drafted from the Crossref records, with a `note` naming the deposits); `run_citations.r --source Gonzalez_2025 --bib` (live Crossref for the records not in the merged worktree caches, 8.5 min) rewrote the generated file with exactly those two entries removed (570 -> 568, 11 reuse a curated key) and left `primary_references.csv` of Gonzalez_2025 unchanged (`bibcite`, `cite_id` kept); the Leahy_2025 reformat restored with `git checkout`. **The owner adds the `BM_citations` rows `Horn_2016` -> `\citep{Horn:2016aa}` and `Rizzuto_2019` -> `\citep{Rizzuto:2019aa}`**; until then RunMe.r warns that the two curated entries have no CiteID mapping (beside the pre-existing `Sereda:2008aa`), and `Bib/TaxonBodyMass_CitationCiteIDs.csv` carries them twice (the curated row with CiteID NA and the `BM_primary_citations` row, `bib_file` now `Citations`).
- Full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off, cache of main f9f16ac): 38,955 species and 10,902 genera as on main; 6 species rows change, all Gonzalez_2025 records of the converted birds and hare (the per-source values move by -0.194 log10 for the birds and -0.068 for the hare, onto the measured wet masses): *Chroicocephalus ridibundus* 265.7 -> 256.0 g, *Haematopus ostralegus* 576 -> 541.3, *Larus canus* 431.5 -> 408.6, *Limosa lapponica* 309 -> 293.9, *Mareca penelope* 752.8 -> 713.2, *Lepus americanus* 1685 -> 1666 (max |dlog10| 0.027 on the species means; `log10_range` falls for four birds, e.g. *Larus canus* 0.324 -> 0.201); the same 6 genus rows; `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 127,215 rows (+6 `conversion_factor` rows citing `Horn_2016` / `Rizzuto_2019`); every other report differs by its timestamp only; `reports/warnings_citations.md`: 0 problems. Tests: `test_citations_provenance.R` (75 checks, the new-group check added) and `test_citations_bib.R` (53) pass.

## 2026-10-05 -- Hoehler_etal_2023: Hudson-coded rows dropped (issue #66) and Stage 2 round (issue #1; Phases A and B)

(2013)' survive the parser's filters (893 Aves, 604 Mammalia, 132 species); 1,235 are row-identical (taxon + mass) to Hudson Appendix S5 (604 of 604 mammals, 631 of 893 birds) and the other 262 bird masses each occur in S5 under another species (43 species; exactly 10 rows of every species with more than 10 Hudson rows -- a block shift in the copy). Implemented as the issue proposed, through `DropImputed()` (reason names the Hudson_2013 duplication and the 262 misaligned masses; `audit/imputed_rows.csv`: 1,497 dropped, 132 taxa, 2,916 kept), all 1,497 rows (the mammal copies included). The registry edge `Hoehler_etal_2023 -> Hudson_2013` becomes `provenance_only` (documentation, no collapse; `n_rows_child` 0, evidence updated) rather than being deleted -- owner's call to retire it instead. Dedupe: `Hoehler_etal_2023 -> Hudson_2013` shared 127 -> 75, identical 36 -> 3; `-> Makarieva_2008` identical 722 -> 742, within tolerance 603 -> 623 (the Hudson rows no longer dilute the mixed species); 45,563 of 124,770 per-source values collapsed. Aptenodytes patagonicus (range 2.38 -> 1.48) and Diomedea exulans (3.05, unchanged) stay out of the species table for other sources' values (VertNet 20 g and Tsuboi_etal_2018 1,318 g for the king penguin; Raymond_2011 8.2 g for the wandering albatross): register candidates for #34, not appended here (pre-existing, not caused by this change).
