The weak matches are a parse artefact of the PNAS reference style 'A. M. Makarieva et al., Title ...' (initials-first author list): `ParseCitationString()` takes the first initial as the author and the surname plus 'et al.,' as the start of the title, so the correct Crossref records of Makarieva 2008 (c1 10.1073/pnas.0802148105), Savage 2004 (10.1111/j.0269-8463.2004.00856.x), Fuentes 2013 (10.1186/1475-2859-12-42) and Bennett & Ruben 1979 (10.1126/science.493968; the source's title typo 'verebrates' and 'Science206' add to it) score 0.71-0.83 -- tooling follow-up (an initials-first style parser, as `ParseNatureStyle()` for Chown), the parsed fields were not edited by hand. Lockyer 1981 is an FAO 'Mammals in the Seas' chapter without a DOI (Crossref's candidate is a 2023 book chapter; `nodoi` candidate); Schonheit et al. 1980 has the right Crossref record as c1 (10.1007/bf00414356) at 0.66 because Crossref garbles the subscripts of its title.
- Roles (owner column of `primary_references.csv`, as instructed): `Savage et al. (2004)`, `Gillooly et al. (2017)`, `Nagy et al. (1999)` set to `compilation` (metabolic-rate compilations whose reference lists are not ingested) -> `compilation_terminal`. Left as `measurement` pending the owner: Weibel 2004 (an MMR compilation, 58 rows), Makarieva 2005 (prokaryote compilation, 83), Tijhuis 1993 (microbial maintenance-energy compilation, 33), Bennett & Ruben 1979 (review, 4), Lockyer 1981 (whale energy budgets, 24).
- Screening: Scite MCP not called (quota exhausted until 2026-11-01); Consensus confirmed all 10 certain DOIs and the five candidate-1 DOIs of the queued rows by title, first author, year and journal, and found Lockyer 1981 as a DOI-less entry (15 DOIs; 14 rows appended to `Bib/scite_checks.csv`, `consensus-mcp`, `unchecked` -- 10.1098/rspb.2016.2328 was already screened in the Wisnionski round; `--verify` re-run from the cache so `services` records the screen). Crossref `update-to` / OpenAlex `is_retracted`: no notice on any. **Scite re-screening due after 2026-11-01.**
- Full run after `--verify` (recompile = TRUE, downloads off): species and genus tables as above (the species table's `source_dependencies` column also reflects the `provenance_only` edge); `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 127,561 rows for 38,967 species, 520 distinct primary CiteIDs; Hoehler_etal_2023 1,888 rows, 1,636 species, 2,565 record links, 0 % resolved until `--bib`: Makarieva 2008 1,100 links `compiled_from` pending, Savage 2004 946 `compilation_terminal` pending, Gillooly 2017 206 and Nagy 1999 127 `compilation_terminal` certain, 178 `compiled_from` the seven certain measurement papers (Makarieva 2005 83, Weibel 55, Tijhuis 33, Scholten 4, Cordova, Long, Schulz 1 each), 8 not found (Lockyer; the other Lockyer whales and Worthy's Orcinus orca are species the range filter removes). `reports/warnings_citations.md`: 1 problem (10 accepted references await `--bib`), 37 references pending / not found database-wide. Hop 2: no Hudson-coded row remains, so nothing routes through `Hudson_2013`; the Makarieva route waits for the owner's decision on the queued row (a scratch run with that row set to `approved` (file and outputs restored afterwards) routed its 1,095 record links of 872 species to `compilation_terminal` at `Makarieva_2008` (hop 1, `primary_cite_id Makarieva_2008`, via the DOI route of `MatchIntermediateLabel()`), i.e. the terminal rule, because `Makarieva_2008` has no `primary_references.csv` yet; they become hop-2 rows by themselves once its reference lists are ingested).

## 2026-10-05 -- Ikeda_2014 (existing source, Stage 2 of issue #1; Phases A and B, no PR)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-1-stage2-ikeda` from main f9f16ac).

- Source: Ikeda (2014) Respiration and ammonia excretion by marine metazooplankton taxa, Mar. Biol. 161:2753 (10.1007/s00227-014-2540-5; the ESM tables S1-S3, `parse_ikeda_esm.py` -> `ikeda2014_esm_parsed.csv`). Phase A: the parser fix of the 2026-10-03 wave (#2, commits 52b8fd0 / 7e38514) had landed -- the ESM's Reference column survives as `ref` and the R parser carried it as `ref_keys`; the parser now does so through `SplitRefKeys(adat$ref, ';')` (the convention of the other sources; one key per record, so the separator never occurs). Key form: the tables' author-year text, 'Ikeda (2013a)', 'Ikeda and Mitchell (1982)', 'Ikeda et al. (2007)', 'Ikeda (unpublished data)'; 792 of 792 kept records keyed, 37 distinct keys (Ikeda et al. 2007 181 records, Ikeda 2014a 105, 2014b 78, 2013d 70, 2013a 63, 2013b 58, Ikeda et al. 2001 54, 2013c 42, Ikeda & Takahashi 2012 28, Ikeda 1974 19, Cetta et al. 1986 15, Biggs 1977 13, ... 11 keys with a single record). The cached frame is unchanged in every column (`ref_keys` values identical); species and genus tables byte-identical to main.
- Reference list: the ESM has none, so the list is the one the paper's Crossref record deposits (101 references, 89 with DOI; the 'closed world' of the plan). Tooling: `--init` for the spec format `crossref_reflist` was not implemented (it stopped with 'not implemented yet'); added `ParseAuthorYearKey()` and `ReflistFromCrossrefReferences()` in `parse_reflists.r` (the key's first surname with diacritics folded, year, letter suffix and author form -- one author, the two surnames, three or more for 'et al.' -- against the author block of the deposited `unstructured` text; a key matching no or several deposited references is reported unresolved; the deposited text is `raw_citation`, the deposited DOI `raw_doi`, the Crossref reference key the `note`; a chapter 'In: ... (eds)' is flagged `owner_review`), the `crossref_reflist` branch of `--init` in `run_citations.r` (`CompilationDOI()` moved before it), `compilation_doi` in the `Ikeda_2014` spec (the curated `Ikeda:2014aa` entry has no doi field), 9 new checks in `test_citations_parse.R` and 2 in `test_citations_services.R` (on the recorded Ikeda 2014 record). All 37 keys join: 36 published (34 with DOI; Ikeda 1974 and Roger 1988 without) and the unpublished key kept as `self`.
- `--init --verify --queue` (live Crossref + OpenAlex with the owner's key; 75 requests -- 37 Crossref, 38 OpenAlex -- cached under `sources/citations_cache/`): certain 33 (`doi_resolves`: every one through the deposited DOI, both services resolve it and agree on first author and year; none needed the open search or the title-similarity closed-world rule), self 1 ('Ikeda (unpublished data)', 3 records), pending 1 (`owner_review`: Bamstedt 1979, a chapter in Naylor & Hartnoll eds. Cyclic phenomena in marine plants and animals, Pergamon, 2 records; candidate 1 = the deposited DOI 10.1016/B978-0-08-023217-1.50041-2, title_sim 1.00, both services), not_found 2 (`below_threshold`: Ikeda 1974 Mem. Fac. Fish. Hokkaido Univ. 22: 1-97, 19 records, best candidate a 2020 fish-farming chapter at 0.55; Roger 1988 Oceanol. Acta 11: 107-116, 2 records, best candidates three 2026 French theses at 0.52); 3 rows queued. Records resolved 769 of 792 (97.1%).
- Screening: Scite MCP quota exhausted until 2026-11-01 (not called); Consensus confirmed by title, author, year and journal all 27 certain DOIs not screened in earlier rounds (6 were: Ikeda & Mitchell 1982, Ikeda & Bruce 1986, Ikeda & Skjoldal 1989, Ikeda et al. 2001, Iguchi & Ikeda 2004, Gorsky et al. 1987) and the Bamstedt 1979 candidate; 28 rows added to `Bib/scite_checks.csv` (`consensus-mcp`, `unchecked`); `--verify --force` marked the 34 rows `;consensus-mcp`. Crossref `update-to` / OpenAlex `is_retracted`: no notice. Consensus indexes Torres et al. 1994 and Thuesen & Childress 1993 under their Oceanographic Literature Review reprints (1995 / 1993) and lists an undated manuscript copy of Ikeda 2013c ('Unknown Journal', 2018); the DOIs held are the journal articles. **Scite re-screening due after 2026-11-01.**
- Bib keys: six of the 33 DOIs already have entries (`Ikeda:1982aa`, `Ikeda:1986aa`, `Ikeda:1989aa`, `Ikeda:2001aa`, `Iguchi:2004aa`, `Gorsky:1987aa`), reused by DOI at `--bib`; the other 27 would be new (Ikeda 2013a-d and 2014a-b will take `Ikeda:2013aa`-`ad` / `Ikeda:2014ab`-`ac` beside the curated `Ikeda:2014aa`). Not run (Phase C).
- Owner decisions (2026-10-05, recorded in `Bib/pending_citations.csv`, `decided_by owner`): Bamstedt (1979) `1` (the chapter DOI 10.1016/B978-0-08-023217-1.50041-2); Ikeda (1974) `nodoi` (owner's blanket policy from this round on: every work no service indexes becomes a DOI-less entry built from the `parsed_*` fields, here taken from the deposited citation -- Ikeda, 1974, Nutritional ecology of marine zooplankton, Mem Fac Fish Hokkaido Univ 22: 1-97; `MatchingNoDOIEntry()` may unify it with Kiorboe_2013 reference 5 / Hebert_etal_2016 reference 69 if those are approved the same way, their title being identical); Roger (1988) `nodoi` (Roger, 1988, Recyclage des sels nutritifs par le macroplancton-micronecton dans le Pacifique tropical Sud-Ouest, Oceanol Acta 11: 107-116). Applied in Phase C below. Omori & Ikeda 1984 does not recur (not an ESM key).
- Full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off; `numbers_db.tex` write suppressed in the tmp copy): `TaxonBodyMass.csv` and `TaxonBodyMass_GenusLevel.csv` byte-identical to main (38,955 species, 10,902 genera); `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 127,209 -> 127,236 rows, only the Ikeda_2014 and Vanni_2017 labels differ. Ikeda_2014: 683 rows of 297 species (339 `compiled_from` hop 1 -- 331 certain, 2 pending, 6 not_found --, 1 `measured_in_source`, 343 `conversion_factor`), 0% with a CiteID until `--bib` (585 record links, `uningested` 585 -> 0). ## 2026-10-05 -- Screening policy change (owner decision 2026-10-05; tooling, issue #114 and #1)

- Policy: a reference is `certain` on Crossref + OpenAlex agreement with clean Crossref `update-to` and OpenAlex `is_retracted` fields alone. A row in `Bib/scite_checks.csv` is not required for `certain`, and a missing row or a `none` row never changes a status (the `pending` / `unscreened` flip of 2026-10-04 is retired; `unscreened` stays in the vocabulary for the queue rows written before). Scite (Consensus as its fallback) is used selectively: (a) a DOI whose services carry a retraction or correction notice, to read the notice type; (b) rows where the two services disagree or only one answered (`single_service`, `ambiguous`, an accepted row with one service); (c) doubtful identities (grey literature, a DOI shared by several keys, a mismatching source DOI, references before 1960); (d) a random audit sample of the newly certain DOIs per round (5%, at least 3). A screening row that reports a retraction or correction still forces `pending` / `retracted`. The `owner-waiver` row of the Smith_2003 round is no longer needed and stays valid for history (its 30 rows kept).
- Tooling (branch `issue-114-selective-screening`): `NoticeFor()` / `DecideMatch()` and `ApplySciteChecks()` no longer treat a gap as a notice; the overlay removes the earlier rule's `;none` suffix and `screening:none` note from a row the next time it runs. New `ScreeningCandidates()` (`decide.r`) lists the DOIs to screen for a source with the categories notice / disagreement / doubtful / audit_sample, the sample drawn under the seed `strtoi(substr(sha1("<Src> <date>"), 1, 7), 16)` so that a round's draw can be repeated (`citations_screening` in `citations_config.r`: `sample_frac 0.05`, `min_sample 3`, `old_year 1960`); exposed as `run_citations.r --screening-list`, which writes `reports/screening_<Src>.md` and nothing else (no network). README screening section rewritten. Tests: `test_citations_decide.R` 85 -> 98 checks (the two `unscreened` assertions replaced, the stale-note cleanup and the screening list added), `test_citations_services.R` 65 unchanged.
- Effect on the tracked files (offline re-verification from the response cache for 10 sources, the overlay alone for the 11 whose cache misses a query; 7,385 rows): **0 status changes**. The 7 rows whose latest screening row is `none` and that the old rule would have turned to `pending` / `unscreened` at their next `--verify` now keep their status: Brose_etal_2018 Jacob_2015 (approved), Lislevand_etal_2007 33 and 40 (approved), Tobias_2022 "Galbraith & Galbraith (1962)" (approved), Wilman_etal_2014 Ref_128 and Ref_164 (approved) and Ref_174 (certain). Field-only cleanups at the next `--verify` of each source: 26 rows lose the stale `screening:none` note (Smith_2003 77-92 and 96-101, Lislevand_etal_2007 33 and 40, Brose_etal_2018 Jacob_2015, Brose_2005 Jacob_unpublished) and the two `;none` suffixes go (Brose_2005 Jacob_unpublished; Kiorboe_2013 9 keeps its stale suffix, having no DOI); 140 rows gain the `;consensus-mcp` / `;owner-waiver` suffix their screening rows already entitle them to under either rule. ## 2026-10-06 -- Makarieva_2008 (existing source, Stage 2 of issue #1; Phase A only, no PR)

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-1-stage2-makarieva` from main 8b1ff08; worktree seeded from the dataretriever worktree's `sources/Rdata`).

- Source: Makarieva et al. (2008) PNAS 105:16994 (10.1073/pnas.0802148105), whose SI (`pnas08SI.pdf`, 212 pages, the authors' mirror http://www.bioticregulation.ru/common/pdf/pnas08/pnas08SI.pdf; the copy on disk in the scratch worktree was used, the file stays untracked and is now in `.gitignore`) holds one dataset per taxonomic group with its own reference list and cites a record's source differently in every table. `parse_makarieva_si.py` (PyMuPDF; the landscape pages are rotated 90 degrees, so a cell's start is its `y1`; left-edge columns for the justified Source cells of S1a/S4/S7 whose words are pushed to the column's right edge, nearest-centre columns for S3; records straddling a page break; the S3 marks are 6.5 pt superscripts; S6b reference cells wrap below their row, a species sits above or below its values, decimal commas) writes `references.csv` (619 entries: the lists of Tables S1a 227, S2a 124 (Russian style, with 'I941' misprints and Cyrillic look-alike initials cleaned for parsing only), S3 115 (byte-identical to Chown_etal_2007's Appendix S2 list), S4 88, S6b 21, S7 40, plus Chown et al. 2007, White et al. 2006, FishBase and McKechnie & Wolf 2004 as the dataset notes cite them; keys `<table>:<author-year>` from each entry's author block, the S3 numbers `S3:n`; a key two entries share becomes `<key> [n]`), `record_refs.csv` (2,691 rows, one per row of the committed S*.csv tables, joined by position with the species as a check; `BodyMass_Makarieva_etal_2008.r` reads it into `ref_keys` through `SplitRefKeys()`), and `S2a_parsed.csv`, `S3_parsed.csv` (with the green flag), `S6b_parsed.csv`. The frame is unchanged in every existing column (2,610 records; 81 table rows have no numeric mass or a placeholder name). Keying per table: S1a/S7 the Source study and the bracketed cell-size source (`BM`/`BM9` = Bergey's Manuals -> Holt 1984 / Holt et al. 1994; an image or shape gives no key); S2b the Table S2a row with the species' minimum qWkg and Mpg; S3 the 391 black rows to `S3:Chown et al. 2007` (388 match a Chown_etal_2007 record by species and mass, superscripts identical to Chown's marks on all 388; routed to `Chown_etal_2007` at hop 2 through the DOI of `Chown:2007aa`), the 11 green rows (the authors' additions; the SI says ten) to their numbered reference; S4 the Source study plus a 'data of X' study the list holds ('Wieser 1963, 1965' two keys); S5a/b `S5:White et al. 2006`; S5c `S5c:FishBase`; S6a MW rows `S6:McKechnie & Wolf 2004`, G rows the references of the S6b row of that species with the minimum night-time rate (reproduces every one of the 110 values); S6b one key per Gavrilov paper ('Gavrilov, 1980abc, 1982ab' -> five). 2,610 of 2,610 records keyed, 449 with several keys. 21 record keys needed a documented fallback (year letter -> list position for Edwards & Lloyd 1977, Marshall 1948, Zeledon 1960, flagged `owner_review`; same authors with the year off by one or missing; abbreviated author lists; the only lettered entry; near-identical spellings -- all listed in the README), 19 keys (35 records) are in no SI list and stay unmatched.
- Faults of the committed tables found on the way (record set left as is; owner's call): 18 `S4.csv` rows mis-split so that `Mg` holds a neighbouring column (Arietellus, Bathycalanus, Megacalanus, Gaussia princeps, Idotea baltica, Cleistosoma edwardsii, Gennadas kempi, Crossota, Pantachogon rows); `S6b.csv` lacks 11 of Table S6b's 218 rows and truncates the multi-line reference cells; `S1a.csv` row 245 ('Yersinia pestis') holds the rest of the page in `valid_name`; `S3.csv` drops trailing numerals of 'Species 2' / 'Griburius sp. 1'-type names; `S2b.csv` Podophrya fixa qWkg 2.9 vs Table S2a 0.52.
- `reflist_specs$Makarieva_2008` (csv, folder `Makarieva_etal_2008`, frame `Makarieva_2008`, `type_col note`, `review_col owner_review`, compiler Makarieva); `--init --offline`: 340 native keys (S1a 148, S4 86, S2a 46, S7 31, S6b 20, S3 6, S5/S5c/S6 one each), 19 not in the list, 298 list entries cited by no kept record, 5 rows `owner_review` (the four letter-mapped S2a entries, FishBase); no `self` key. Roles set by the round (owner column): `compilation` for `S3:Chown et al. 2007` (391 records), `S5:White et al. 2006` (314), `S6:McKechnie & Wolf 2004` (211), `S4:Seibel 2007` (38), `S1a:Holt 1984` (65), `S1a:Holt et al. 1994` (23); `database` for `S5c:FishBase` (266); `parsed_year` supplied for the three S2a entries printed with a capital I. Registry note updated; `sources/databases/Makarieva_etal_2008/README.md` written (the source had none). Tests `test_citations_parse.R` (79) and `test_citations_provenance.R` (84) pass.
- Full run (recompile = TRUE, downloads off, `numbers_db.tex` write suppressed in `tmp/RunMe_offline.r`): `TaxonBodyMass.csv` and `TaxonBodyMass_GenusLevel.csv` byte-identical to main 8b1ff08 (39,023 species); `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 172,589 -> 173,068 rows; Makarieva_2008 1,267 -> 1,746 rows of 1,267 species, 2,698 record links (878 `compiled_from` hop 1 of 399 + 25 species, 777 `compilation_terminal` -- Chown / White / McKechnie & Wolf / Seibel / Holt -- of 752 + 25 species, 91 `database_record` FishBase), every `match_status` NA until `--verify`. (2008)' stay `compilation_terminal` at `Makarieva_2008` for now; expected after verification: 493 rows (494 links) of species whose Makarieva records cite a compilation-role key become hop-2 rows at White et al. 2006 / McKechnie & Wolf 2004 / Seibel 2007 (terminal) or at `Chown_etal_2007` (the S3 rows; no third hop), 281 rows (498 links) hop-2 rows at the measurement references as they turn certain, 24 rows (28 links) both, 73 rows (74 links) stay terminal (species without a kept Makarieva record). Ehnes_etal_2011 and Baach_2026 copy Makarieva values without a key to it (`Bib/source_dependencies.csv`), so nothing routes from them.

Tooling `tbmcite 0.1.0` (branch `issue-1-stage2-faurby` from main 8b1ff08; `sources/Rdata` seeded from the issue-69 DataRetriever cache, `BodyMass_DataRetrieverAll.Rdata` md5 3900da12...).

Body mass of late Quaternary mammals. Ecology 84:3403 (Updated Version 4.1 obtained from senior author).' on 3,421 kept rows, or a bare 'Journal volume: pages (year)' string without author or title; 620 spellings over the extant rows), `Mass.Method` (Reported 4,585; Assumed isometric based on head-body length 357, forearm length 201, skull size 87, mandible size 26, and nine rarer dimensions, 685 in all; Estimated based on equation from skull or maximum length 3; Imputed 186; As relative of suggested similar size 18), `Mass.Comparison` and `Mass.Comparison.Source` (the reference species and its source on the allometry rows). The parser already drops the `Imputed` and `As relative` rows (204; `DropImputed()`, `audit/imputed_rows.csv`) and keeps 5,273, so the no-imputed-values rule holds and no record set changes: no owner decision is needed on imputed rows. (The brief's count of 5,169 species does not match the file: 5,273 rows are kept and the database accepts 4,998 Faurby species.)
- Parser: `BodyMass_Faurby_etal_2018.r` keeps `ref_keys` as the hash key `h:<sha1-8>` of the normalised `Mass.Source` string (`ParseInRowCitations()`, the Herberstein precedent) and the record-level override `prov_type = 'derived_allometry'` on the 688 allometry rows (the first parser to use the override; `Mass.Source` there is the work that measured the species' dimension, `Mass.Comparison` the reference species -- 428 of the 437 are PHYLACINE species themselves -- whose mass:dimension ratio was applied; the same work as `Mass.Comparison.Source` on 470 rows, another work on 215 rows, 40 of those works cited nowhere as a `Mass.Source`; the comparison source is not kept as a key). Frame rebuilt and compared with the cached one: 5,273 rows, taxon, mass_g, n, source_mass, order, family byte-identical; 5,273 keyed (0 NA), 596 distinct keys (nine fold two spellings: 'Global Ecology and Biogeography 18 :19-29 (2009)' / '18: 19-29').
- `reflist_specs$Faurby_etal_2018` (format `inrow`, file `Trait_data.csv`, `citation_col = 'Mass.Source'`, compiler Faurby, `compilation_doi` 10.1002/ecy.2443). `--init --offline`: 596 keys -> `primary_references.csv`, every key in the list; 92 references of the file belong to dropped rows ('PHYLACINE 1.2' on the imputed ones) and are not verified; 0 `self`; 1 key with a DOI in its text (Trillmich 2015, the IUCN Red List assessment of *Zalophus wollebaeki*). Records per key: 1 key x 3,421 (Smith 2003), 52 keys with 5 or more (Smith et al. 2008 China 115, Garbutt 2007 101, 'Global Ecology and Biogeography 18: 19-29 (2009)' 91, Aulagnier et al. 2009 77, Francis 2008 67, Van Dyck & Strahan 2008 49, Eisenberg & Redford 2000 38, 'Global Ecology and Biogeography 17: 274-283 (2008)' 32, Reid 2009 32, 'Journal of Mammalogy 89: 852-873 (2008)' 31, 'Journal of Biogeography 34: 1439-1454 (2007)' 31, ...), 149 keys x 2-4, 395 keys x 1. Hop-2 candidates: the Smith 2003 key, 3,421 records (3,208 accepted species).
- Roles (owner column, the standing rule of the Smith_2003 round): `compilation` for Smith et al. 2003 and 92 handbooks, field guides, encyclopaedias, regional monographs and faunal databases (the HMW volumes, Smith et al. 2008, Garbutt 2007, Aulagnier et al. 2009, Francis 2008, Van Dyck & Strahan 2008, Eisenberg & Redford 2000 and 1989, Reid 2009, Flannery 1994 and 1995, Ohdachi et al. 2009, National Audubon 1996, Nowak 1999, the 43 species accounts of Happold's Mammals of Africa, Gardner 2008, Patton et al. 2015, Corbet & Hill 1992, Corbet 1978, Kingdon 2003, Gromov & Polyakov 1977, Gromov & Erbajeva 1995, Shenbrot et al. 1995, Emmons & Feer 1990, Kays & Wilson 2009, Stuart & Stuart 2007, Jefferson et al. 1993, Bottoto-Paez & Mancina 2011, Flannery et al. 1996, Dixon 1998, Campbell et al. 2007, Flores et al. 2008 in the Encyclopedia of Marine Mammals; the IUCN fact sheets and Red List assessment, the Field Museum checklists 'Mammals of Tanzania' and 'Synapsis of Philippine Mammals' (sic), the Smithsonian North American Mammals database, EDGE, the Western Bat Working Group), 93 keys, 4,207 record links; `measurement` for the rest (503 keys, 1,066 links: journal articles, survey papers, theses, chapters). `owner_review` set on 11 keys: 4 theses (Gabor 2008, 18 records; Bronner; 'Keamey' (sic); Mekonnen 2007), 4 edited volumes cited without the chapter (Tarsiers 2003, the J. Knox Jones memorial volume, The Quintessential Naturalist, Developments in Primatology), 'Information kindly provided by Robert Bradley. Texas Tech University' (personal communication), 'Forearm larger than 44 mm following Royal Ontario Museum ...' (a note, not a citation) and Meng et al. 2016 (a Scientific Data descriptor; role to confirm).
- Surprise, for the owner before `--verify`: 486 of the 596 keys (1,180 records) give no author or title -- 'Journal of Mammalogy 83: 1-19 (2002)', 'Bonner Zoologische Beitraege 43: 383-392 (1992)', 'Annals and Magazine of Natural History 8 12: 395-403 (1912)' (series and volume) -- and the book strings 'Garbutt N. Mammals of Madagascar a Complete Guide (2007)' are read by `ParseCitationString()` with the whole head as `parsed_author1` and no `parsed_title`, `parsed_container`, `parsed_volume` or `parsed_pages`. `--verify` then queries the raw string and cannot reach `certain` (every rule rests on `title_sim`), so these keys will come back `not_found` / `below_threshold` or `no_candidates`; only the 110 keys with a title (4,093 records, the Smith key among them) can be decided by the current rules. Options: (a) a parse for the 'Container volume: pages (year)' shape plus a decision rule accepting a Crossref candidate whose container, volume, first page and year all agree (an addition to issue #1, 2.3, owner decision); (b) `parsed_*` filled by hand for the high-count keys; (c) leave them unresolved (17% of the record links, mostly singletons). Option (a) chosen by the owner the same day (next bullet).
- Tooling (owner decision 2026-10-06, after merging main bb3a9f5 with the #121 follow-ups): `ParseJournalOnlyStyle()` in `normalise_citation.r`, tried after `ParseNatureStyle()`, reads the title-less shapes 'Journal volume: pages (year)', 'Journal series volume: pages (year)' (the series number is dropped), 'Journal volume pages (year)' without a colon, a year-numbered volume ('Proceedings of the Zoological Society of London 1906 859-864 (1906)'), a year range ('(1948-1949)' -> 1948), several page ranges (the first is kept) and a trailing note in brackets; a head holding a year or an author block ('Doe JA, Roe B. A study. J Things 5: 1-10 (2001)') is left to the generic parse. Two small fixes for the book form: initials closed by a period ('Wozencraft WD.') end the author block (`SplitAuthorsFromTitle()`), and a bracketed note after the bracketed year is dropped. `DecideMatchWith()` gains the rule `container_volume_page` for a reference without `parsed_title`: a candidate whose container (abbreviation-aware), volume, first page and year (within the window) all agree is `certain` / `container_volume_page` when Crossref and OpenAlex both return it, `pending` / `single_service` from one service, `pending` / `ambiguous` when two DOIs agree, `not_found` / `below_threshold` (or `grey_literature`) when none does; a notice still gives `retracted`, and a decision without OpenAlex is `service_unavailable` like the other agreement rules. Vocabulary (`match_reasons`) and `R/library/citations/README.md` (file table, decision-rule table) updated. Tests: `test_citations_parse.R` 92 -> 114 checks (15 Faurby strings, the book form, the NULL cases), `test_citations_decide.R` 112 -> 126 (the rule's outcomes); all six files pass (89 / 126 / 114 / 84 / 65 / 25). `primary_references.csv` rebuilt from scratch (`--init --offline`, roles and `owner_review` re-applied by the same rule, `--init` again for the report): of the 481 title-less keys (1,022 records) 471 (990 records) now carry container, volume, pages and year; 10 (32 records) keep an unparsable shape ('Journal of Mammalogy 86: 873: 880 (2005)', 'Records of the Western Australian Museum 17: 1:28 (1995)', '46: 145-169 (1995' without its bracket, 'Acta Oecologica 41-49 (2012)', the National Audubon guide, the Bronner thesis, the forearm note, Tree Kangaroos, Gardner 2008, Barrett 2010); 115 keys (4,251 records) have a title. Verification tonight on the owner's go.
- Full run (`recompile = TRUE`, downloads off, `ms_dir` pointed at `tmp/ms`; `DataSheets = FALSE`): `TaxonBodyMass.csv` and `TaxonBodyMass_GenusLevel.csv` byte-identical to main 8b1ff08 (39,023 species); `TaxonBodyMass_Provenance.csv.gz` 172,589 -> 172,620 rows. Faurby_etal_2018: 5,029 rows for 4,998 accepted species (5,046 record links; 30 species have two rows, synonyms of one accepted species citing different keys): `compilation_terminal` 3,801 (3,224 citing the Smith key, 577 the handbooks), `compiled_from` 549, `derived_allometry` 679 (the override; 491 citing a journal article, 188 a handbook); every `match_status` NA (unverified), `pct_resolved` 0. Smith_2003 (4,219 rows) and Wilman_etal_2014 (3,744 rows through Smith) unchanged. `reports/warnings_citations.md`: 0 problems, 5,640 unverified references (596 of them Faurby's).
- Expected routing after `--verify` (the Smith key resolves to 10.1890/02-9003 at both services; `Smith:2003aa` carries the DOI, so `MatchIntermediateLabel()` routes hop 2): of the 3,208 accepted species citing Smith, 3,124 have a resolved Smith_2003 reference -- about 3,739 `compiled_via_compilation` rows via `Smith_2003` (3,497 through Smith's compilation-role keys, 242 through its measurement keys; `Silva_1995` 817, `Jones_2003` 575, `Marquet_1999` 423, `Ernest_2003b` 203, `Strahan_1995` 180, `Kingdon_1997` 149, `Brown_1991` 120 first) -- and 84 stay `compilation_terminal` at `Smith_2003`; the 92 handbook keys become `compilation_terminal` with a primary citation once approved (`nodoi` by the standing policy, none is indexed); the 679 override rows keep `derived_allometry` whatever their key's status. Dependencies (`Bib/source_dependencies.csv`, Faurby -> Smith_2003 copies, tolerance 0.0105) unchanged.

## 2026-10-06 -- Owner decision workflow: recommendations, the Sheet tab BM_decisions, push (issue #1)

- Tooling (owner decision 2026-10-06, branch `issue-1-decisions-sheet` from main 6322e33): the owner decides the open queue rows on a Sheet tab instead of the CSV or a prose batch. `Bib/pending_citations.csv` gains `recommendation`, `recommendation_reason`, `recommended_by`, `owner_note` (optional; a 38-column file reads with them NA; `DedupeQueue()` ignores them); `QueueDecisionProblems()` factored out of `ApplyQueueDecisions()`, which also copies a non-empty `owner_note` into the row's `notes`. `recommend.r`: `--recommend` (per source, or `--all-sources` with `reports/recommendations_<date>.md`) fills a recommendation per open row under thirteen ordered rules (README, section "Recommendations"; twin publisher DOIs -> the top-scored, incomplete fields with no good candidate -> `drop`, both owner policies of 2026-10-06; agent rows kept unless `--force`). `decisions_sheet.r`: `--decisions-push` appends one row per open queue row to `BM_decisions` (key source_label + native_key + queued_at; the parsed citation on one line, HYPERLINK candidates `title (year) container [sim]`, the recommendation, empty `decision` / `owner_note` / `status`; dry run by default; snapshot `Bib/BM_decisions_snapshot.csv`; a non-strict dropdown `accept | 1 | 2 | 3 | nodoi | drop | self | defer` on `decision` through the Sheets `batchUpdate`) and the questions of `Bib/decision_items.csv` to `BM_decision_items`; `--decisions-pull` reads both back, validates every decision per row (`accept` = the shown recommendation; grammar, `QueueDecisionProblems()`, a `doi:` at Crossref), writes the sound ones into the queue (`decided_by owner`, `decided_at` the pull date) and the problems into the tab's `status` column, never stopping; then the per-source `--apply-queue --bib --sheet` as before. Tests: `test_citations_recommend.R` 41, `test_citations_decisions_sheet.R` 47 (fake Sheet, no network), `test_citations_decide.R` 183 -> 193; all eight citation files pass.
- `--recommend --all-sources` on the 968 open rows: 693 with a recommendation, 275 left to the owner. Rule x source: titleless_journal_key 263 (Faurby, awaiting #128), jstor_twin 29 (Jones), publisher_twin 66 (Jones), strong_candidate 86 (Jones 84, Meiri 2), grey_complete 5, fields_complete_no_match 391, fields_incomplete 67, titleless_incomplete 6, owner_review_flagged 4 (Faurby chapters); overlay from the residue batch (`recommended_by agent`, 51 rows): Makarieva_2008 22 (section 2a: `1` x 8 incl. the two correction notices, `doi:` x 2, `nodoi` x 5, 3 "owner check", 4 "see item Makarieva_2008-3"), Myhrvold_2015 7 (section 3), Meiri_2018 Oliver and McDonald 2016 `doi:10.1098/rsos.160018` (a Correction; the queue row has no candidate, so `1` cannot apply), Jones_2009 21 of the 56 rows where the batch's group label disagrees with the policy (the same title in the same journal with a re-deposited record date, 14 J. Cetacean Res. Manage. 2001 papers dated 2020: `1:year=2001`; a small year gap: `1`); the other 35 keep the policy (JSTOR book reviews of the cited book, "twins" that are book chapters decades away, pairs under 0.90). Recommendations: `1` 187, `1:year=` 15, `2` 8, `doi:` 4, `nodoi` 403, `drop` 76, empty 275. Every recommendation passes the grammar and the apply-time checks.
- `--decisions-push` dry run, then `--no-dry-run`: `BM_decisions` created and 968 rows appended (Faurby_etal_2018 277, Hebert_etal_2016 10, Jones_2009 608, Kiorboe_2013 2, Makarieva_2008 22, Meiri_2018 23, Myhrvold_2015 7, Wilman_etal_2014 19), the decision dropdown set by the API; `BM_decision_items` created with the 8 items (Makarieva_2008 4: Gavrilov role, S1a/S7 dual keys, year-letter mapping, White:2005aa; Faurby_etal_2018 2: HMW vol. 5 year, Nowak fold; Myhrvold_2015 1: literature_cited.csv class; Jones_2009 1: De-Magalhaes DOI). Both snapshots committed (`Bib/BM_decisions_snapshot.csv` 968 rows with the candidates as their display text, `Bib/BM_decision_items_snapshot.csv` 8). The owner's one-time cosmetics (freeze A:B, wrap `parsed`) are not done by code.
- Next: the owner fills `accept` down `decision` on `BM_decisions`, changes the disagreements, answers `BM_decision_items`, says "pull"; `--decisions-pull`, commit `Bib: owner decisions pulled from BM_decisions (<date>)`; then Phase C per source (`--apply-queue --bib`, `--sheet` dry run then real, pipeline, one PR per source, small ones grouped).

- Tooling (`ApplyQueueDecisions()`, `decide.r`): the first `--apply-queue` applied nothing for 2655 and cleared `Nowak:1999ab` / `Nowak_1999b` from Jones_2009 2152. The "already applied" test compared `decided_at` alone, so the `doi:` decision of 2026-10-08 looked like the `nodoi` of the same day, and every decided row of a key was replayed in queue order, so 2152's standing-policy `nodoi` of 2026-10-06 ran again before its `doi:` of 2026-10-08. Now a key's current decision is its last decided row (the earlier ones are history), applied unless the row already carries it (`DecisionOnRow()`: `decided_at` equal and the status, reason and DOI or key the decision produces) or carries a newer owner decision (the Gavrilov `self` rows set by hand under item Makarieva_2008-1 would otherwise be re-decided `nodoi` by their 2026-10-06 queue rows at the next Makarieva `--apply-queue`). The `--apply-queue` report line counts a row whose status, reason, DOI or `decided_at` changed (it read "0 applied" for the two Makarieva `doi:` re-decisions of approved rows). `test_citations_decide.R` 197 -> 201 checks; README paragraph on the queue.
